## 2026-10-27 · Other · Development Shops: Virtual credit cards for mobile test orders

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/9s2dupvc

**Test mobile checkout without using a personal credit card.** Eligible test buyers can select a platform-provided virtual credit card when ordering from a Full Account Development Shop. Payments made with the test card are simulated and do not move real funds.
### What changes

* **Test cards appear in checkout.** Use the payment methods provided for your eligible test shop, without entering a card number, CVV or expiry date.
* **Create test orders from the TikTok app.** Complete the buyer checkout flow, then use the resulting order to verify order handling in your integration.
* **Keep test payments separate from real payments.** Virtual-card payments are simulated. If you select a real bank card instead, the payment follows the real payment flow and may incur charges.

### Availability and scope
Applies to **Full Account Development Shops where test-card payments are enabled**, using the associated TikTok test buyer account. Availability depends on the market and account. Core Account shops do not use this mobile checkout flow.
Test-card orders are for functional testing. They do not support real settlement or payout validation. Cancellation and refund testing depends on the flows enabled for your test shop; virtual-card refunds are simulated and do not return money to a real bank account.
### Quick start guide

1. **Prepare the test shop and buyer account.** Use a Full Account Development Shop with test-card payments enabled. Confirm that your TikTok buyer account is linked to the test shop and marked for testing, then sign in to that account on your phone.
2. **Open a product from the test shop.** Choose Buy now, or check out from the cart. Confirm that the product belongs to the intended Development Shop.
3. **Select the test card.** In Payment or Payment method, choose the platform-provided card for a successful test payment. In the example below, this is Card ending in 4242. Use the card configured for your test shop; the displayed labels and suffixes may vary.
4. **Place the order and check the result.** Tap Place order or Pay now, depending on your checkout page. After payment succeeds, open View order and check that the test order is listed in your Development Shop’s order management and handled correctly by your integration.

**Optional: check cancellation or refund handling.** Where enabled, open the test order in My orders and use the cancellation or refund action available for its current status. Complete any required seller-side action and verify the resulting status. These checks do not validate a real bank refund or settlement.
#### If a test payment does not work

* **Test card missing:** check the buyer account, linked shop, Full Account mode and whether the feature is enabled for your market/account. Do not add a personal card as a workaround for simulated payment testing.
* **Payment fails with a promotion applied:** retry a test order without the promotion. If the issue persists, provide the test shop, market, order ID and screenshot to your usual Partner Center support channel.

#### Mobile checkout example
The example shows the successful-payment test card ending in **4242** selected before placing an order. Shipping details have been redacted. Card presentation and available payment methods may differ by market.

(ảnh: https://p16-arcosite-sg.ibyteimg.com/tos-alisg-i-k9wyc2ijk0-sg/e0e8016a50694d0fa4fa6502adcae619~tplv-k9wyc2ijk0-image.image)

## 2026-10-26 · Products · For ID L2L Market: Tokopedia & TikTok Shop Dual Listing

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/7fcazxi4

### What is changing?
Starting **29 October 2026**, a new Dual Listing policy will take effect for the Indonesia market. Products listed on TikTok Shop will automatically become available on Tokopedia. **For ISVs**, the distribution channel selection for "TikTok Shop-only" will be deprecated in the API.

* **Automatic Dual Listing**: Products currently available only on TikTok Shop will automatically become available for purchase on Tokopedia, subject to platform rules.
* **Channel Consolidation**: When sellers edit or upload products, the default options will be (1) Tokopedia & TikTok Shop or (2) Tokopedia only. **The option for "TikTok Shop-only" will no longer be available.**
* **Unchanged Items**: Products currently listed only on Tokopedia will remain unchanged.
* As part of this policy update, the API logic for product distribution is being simplified. We suggest API integrations should be adjusted to accommodate the removal of the specific TikTok Shop-only distribution channel.

| **Before (Current)** | **After (Effective 29 Oct 2026)** |
| --- | --- |
| Sellers choose the distribution channel (**Toko-only** or **TTS-only**) during import/upload. | **TTS-only** selection is canceled for imported parameters. |
| Verification failures in the selected channel result in total failure. | If policy verification passes for only one channel, the product is published to that channel. The interface returns success. |
### Which markets are affected?
Indonesia, L2L
### What action is required?

* **Update UI Components**: Remove the "TikTok Shop Only" listing option from your seller dashboards/tools.
* **Adjust API Parameters**: Ensure your backend logic handles the deprecated channel parameters and accounts for the new automated distribution behavior.
* **Verify Logic**: Update your system to correctly interpret the successful distribution signals, even in partial channel verification scenarios.

## 2026-10-23 · Finance · Finance  API Updates: New Fee, Tax, Trade-In, and SKU Data

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/62g4f932

#### Summary
This update adds new response fields to the Finance transaction APIs, introduces two new Trade-In account APIs for SEA, and adds SKU details to Search Seller Affiliate Orders. All changes are **backward compatible**. Existing integrations keep working without changes. We recommend adopting the new fields to give sellers a more accurate view of their fees, taxes, and settlements.
| # | Update | Markets | What you get |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 4 new fulfillment fee fields in Finance transaction APIs | US | Itemized hazmat and non-standard parcel fees in each transaction's shipping cost breakdown |
| 2 | `article_22_income_tax_withheld` in Finance transaction APIs | ID | Article 22 income tax withheld by the platform, reported for each transaction |
| 3 | New APIs: Get TradeIn Account Transactions, Get TradeIn Supplier Account Balance | SEA | Trade-In supplier account transactions and available balance |
| 4 | `sku_name` and `seller_sku` in Search Seller Affiliate Orders | All markets | SKU name and seller SKU for each affiliate order SKU |
| 5 | `seller_funded_user_spoint_service_fee` in Finance transaction APIs | All markets | Service fee for seller-funded S-Points, reported for each transaction |

##### 1. [US] Finance Transaction APIs: New Hazmat and Non-Standard Parcel Fees
###### **What is changing?**
Four response fields are added under `shipping_cost_breakdown`. Sellers can now see hazmat and oversize charges as separate line items instead of a single shipping cost.

* `hazmat_noncompliance_fee`: Fee charged when a parcel containing hazardous materials is shipped without the required declaration or compliant packaging, as assessed by the carrier.
* `hazmat_handling_fee`: Fee for the additional handling required to ship a parcel containing hazardous materials.
* `non_standard_weight_fee`: Fee charged when a parcel exceeds the standard weight range. The rate is set by TikTok Shop.
* `non_standard_dimension_fee`: Fee charged when a parcel exceeds the standard size range. The rate is set by TikTok Shop.

All four fields are strings and are charged when the parcel is delivered.
| API | Version | Method and path | Field location |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [Get Transactions by Order](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-transactions-by-order-202501) | 202501 | `GET /finance/202501/orders/{order_id}/statement_transactions` | `sku_transactions[].shipping_cost_breakdown` |
| [Get Transactions by Statement](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-transactions-by-statement-202501) | 202501 | `GET /finance/202501/statements/{statement_id}/statement_transactions` | `transactions[].shipping_cost_breakdown` |
| [Get Unsettled Transactions](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-unsettled-transactions-202507) | 202507 | `GET /finance/202507/orders/unsettled` | `transactions[].shipping_cost_breakdown` |

###### Which markets are affected?
| **Markets** | US. |
| --- | --- |
| **Who is affected** | Developers of ERP, accounting, and finance reconciliation apps, and self-developed sellers who reconcile US shipping costs. |
| **Action** | Recommended, not required. |
| **How to adopt** | 1. Add the four fields to your response models for the API versions you use.  2. Show them as separate shipping fee line items in settlement and profit reports.  3. Handle empty values. These fields are returned empty for markets other than the US. |
###### 2. [ID] Finance Transaction APIs: New Article 22 Income Tax Withheld Field
###### **What is changing?**
One response field, `article_22_income_tax_withheld` (string), is added under `fee_tax_breakdown.tax`. It returns the Article 22 income tax withheld by the platform under PMK 37/2025. Sellers can use it to reconcile settlements against withheld tax.
| API | Version | Method and path | Field location |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [Get Transactions by Order](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-transactions-by-order-202501) | 202501 | `GET /finance/202501/orders/{order_id}/statement_transactions` | `sku_transactions[].fee_tax_breakdown.tax` |
| [Get Transactions by Statement](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-transactions-by-statement-202501) | 202501 | `GET /finance/202501/statements/{statement_id}/statement_transactions` | `transactions[].fee_tax_breakdown.tax` |

###### Which markets are affected?
| **Markets** | Indonesia (ID). |
| --- | --- |
| **Who is affected** | Developers of tax, accounting, and finance reconciliation apps serving Indonesian sellers, and self-developed sellers in Indonesia. |
| **Action** | Recommended, not required. |
| **How to adopt** | 1. Add `article_22_income_tax_withheld` to your tax data model.  2. Show it as a separate line in settlement and tax reports so sellers can match it with their tax records.  3. Handle empty values. This field is returned empty for markets other than Indonesia. |

##### 3. [SEA] New APIs: Get TradeIn Account Transactions and Get TradeIn Supplier Account Balance
###### **What is changing?**
Two new APIs give Trade-In suppliers programmatic access to their Trade-In account. Developers can now automate recharge, payout, and reversal reconciliation instead of checking the account manually.
| API | Method and path | What it returns |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Get TradeIn Account Transactions](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-tradein-account-transactions-202609) (202609) | `GET /finance/202609/account_transactions` | Paginated Trade-In account transactions. Each record includes `transaction_id`, `type` (`RECHARGE` / `WITHDRAW` / `REVERSE`), `direction` (`IN` / `OUT`), `amount`, `create_time`, `reference_id` (Trade-In order ID), and `origin_transaction_id` (for reversals). Filters are `start_time`, `end_time`, and `flow_type`. |
| [Get TradeIn Supplier Account Balance](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-tradein-supplier-account-balance-202609) (202609) | `GET /finance/202609/account_balances` | The `available_balance` (`amount` and `currency`) of the supplier's Trade-In account for the requested `currency`. |

Both APIs require the **Finance Information** scope. The supplier identity is resolved from the authorization context, so you do not pass `seller_id` or `supplier_id`. Only transactions generated by the Trade-In business are returned.
```JSON
{"code":0,"data":{"flows":[{"amount":{"amount":"125.50","currency":"USD"},"create_time":"2026-09-01T10:15:30Z","direction":"IN","reference_id":"TI202609010001","transaction_id":"7894561230123456789","type":"RECHARGE"}],"has_more":true,"next_page_token":"6AsPQ...","total_count":100},"message":"Success"}
```

###### Which markets are affected?
| **Markets** | SEA. |
| --- | --- |
| **Who is affected** | Developers building finance or reconciliation tools for Trade-In suppliers in SEA. |
| **Action** | Optional. Adopt if you serve Trade-In suppliers. |
| **How to adopt** | 1. Make sure your app has the **Finance Information** scope and the supplier has authorized it.  2. Call Get TradeIn Supplier Account Balance to show the supplier's current available balance.  3. Call Get TradeIn Account Transactions with `page_size` and `page_token` to sync transaction history. Continue until `has_more` is `false`.  4. Use `direction` to decide whether each amount is a credit or a debit. Link each `REVERSE` record to its original payout through `origin_transaction_id`.  5. Test with the API Testing Tool in Partner Center before going live. |

##### 4. [All Markets] Search Seller Affiliate Orders: New SKU Name and Seller SKU Fields
###### **What is changing?**
Two string fields are added to each SKU in an affiliate order. You can now map each affiliate order to the seller's own SKU codes without an extra product lookup.

* `sku_name`: The official display name of the SKU, defined and standardized by TikTok Shop. Example: `"Silver PVD"`.
* `seller_sku`: The SKU identifier defined by the seller for their own use. Example: `"cs001-pvd-bag"`.

| API | Version | Method and path | Field location |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [Search Seller Affiliate Orders](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/search-seller-affiliate-orders-202410) | 202410 | `POST /affiliate_seller/202410/orders/search` | `orders[].skus[]` |
###### Which markets are affected?
| **Markets** | All markets. |
| --- | --- |
| **Who is affected** | Developers of affiliate management, creator marketing, and ERP apps, and self-developed sellers who track affiliate sales by SKU. |
| **Action** | Recommended, not required. |
| **How to adopt** | 1. Add `sku_name` and `seller_sku` to your affiliate order SKU model.  2. Use `seller_sku` to join affiliate orders with the seller's inventory and ERP records. |
##### 5. [All Markets] Finance Transaction APIs: New Seller-Funded S-Points Service Fee
**What is changing?**
One response field, `seller_funded_user_spoint_service_fee` (string), is added under `fee_tax_breakdown.fee`. It is the service fee charged for seller-funded S-Points issued to shoppers. The fee is calculated on the order's actual payment amount and refunded if the order is later returned.
| API | Version | Method and path | Field location |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [Get Transactions by Order](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-transactions-by-order-202501) | 202501 | `GET /finance/202501/orders/{order_id}/statement_transactions` | `sku_transactions[].fee_tax_breakdown.fee` |
| [Get Transactions by Statement](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-transactions-by-statement-202501) | 202501 | `GET /finance/202501/statements/{statement_id}/statement_transactions` | `transactions[].fee_tax_breakdown.fee` |
| [Get Unsettled Transactions](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-unsettled-transactions-202507) | 202507 | `GET /finance/202507/orders/unsettled` | `transactions[].fee_tax_breakdown.fee` |
###### Which markets are affected?
| **Markets** | All markets. |
| --- | --- |
| **Who is affected** | Developers of ERP, accounting, and finance reconciliation apps, and self-developed sellers who fund S-Points for shoppers. |
| **Action** | Recommended, not required. |
| **How to adopt** | 1. Add `seller_funded_user_spoint_service_fee` to your fee data model.  2. Show it as a separate promotion cost so sellers can measure the real cost of S-Points campaigns.  3. Handle refunds: the fee is reversed when the order is returned. |
##### Compatibility and support

* All changes are non-breaking. No existing field is removed, renamed, or re-typed, and no request parameter becomes required.
* New fields are additive. Make sure your parser ignores unknown fields or maps them explicitly.
* Questions? [Submit a ticket](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/kwfskwpj) to contact us.

## 2026-10-22 · Orders · All Markets: Order APIs Add LIVE Session ID and Buyer Nickname Fields

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/gfhiex0q

### What is changing?
As of October 22, 2026, two response fields have been added to Order APIs to support LIVE Auction workflows.

* `orders[].line_items[].room_id`: A string containing the LIVE session ID for the order line item. For line items not created from LIVE, this field returns an empty string.
* `orders[].buyer_nickname`: A string containing the TikTok buyer nickname for identifying the buyer in LIVE workflows.

The fields are included in these API specifications:
| API | Version | Method and path | API reference |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Get Order List | 202309 | `POST /order/:version/orders/search` | [Get Order List 202309](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-order-list-202309) |
| Get Order Detail | 202309 | `GET /order/:version/orders` | [Get Order Detail 202309](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-order-detail-202309) |
| Get Order Detail | 202507 | `GET /order/:version/orders` | [Get Order Detail 202507](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-order-detail-202507) |
### Which markets are affected?
This change applies to the following markets: UK, EU, SEA, LATAM, JP.
### Who is affected?
Developers of ERP, OMS, MCM, and connectors applications; and TikTok Shop sellers using LIVE workflows.
### What action is required?

1. Review the response schema for the API version your integration uses.
2. If you intend to consume the new fields, update your response parser and data model for `orders[].line_items[].room_id` and `orders[].buyer_nickname`.
3. Use `room_id` to attribute each LIVE order line item to its LIVE session, and use `buyer_nickname` to identify the buyer in LIVE workflows.
4. Test non-LIVE order behavior: `room_id` returns an empty string when an order line item was not created from a LIVE session.

## 2026-10-22 · Orders · For US, EU, UK, JP markets: New Order Tags in Order APIs

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/l0aungg9

### What is changing?
TikTok Shop Seller Center and app already surfaces order tags that help sellers identify refundable samples, authentication-required orders, subscription orders, and auction orders. This update brings the corresponding order indicators into Open API responses so order-management and fulfillment integrations can interpret the same order context.
Across the listed API versions, three boolean fields are added or extended in `orders[]`, while `AUCTION` is added to `orders[].order_type` only for Get Order Detail `202507`. Market availability and endpoint coverage differ by field, as summarized below.

* `orders[].is_refundable_sample` (`bool`, example: `true`) indicates whether an order is a refundable sample. 
* `orders[].authentication_required` (`bool`, example: `true`) indicates whether an order requires authentication. It is included in all three API schemas. 
* `orders[].is_subscription_order` (`bool`, example: `true`) identifies an order generated from a subscription. It extends support to UK, EU, and JP; US is already supported. 
* `orders[].order_type` (`string`) adds the enum value `AUCTION` for an order placed during an auction where the product price is not fixed. This enum is included only for Get Order Detail `202507`; it is not specified for either `202309` API.

#### API support matrix
| API | `is_refundable_sample` | `authentication_required` | `is_subscription_order` | `order_type=AUCTION` |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Get Order Detail `202507`  `GET /order/:version/orders` | Included | Included | Included   | Included |
| Get Order Detail `202309`  `GET /order/:version/orders` | Included | Included | Included | Not specified |
| Get Order List `202309`  `POST /order/:version/orders/search` | Included | Included | Included | Not specified |
### Which markets are affected?

* `orders[].is_refundable_sample`: US, UK, EU, JP.
* `orders[].authentication_required`: US, UK, EU.
* `orders[].is_subscription_order`: UK, EU, JP; US is already supported.
* `orders[].order_type=AUCTION`: US, UK, EU, JP, LATAM, SEA; limited to Get Order Detail `202507`.

### Who is affected?
Developers and applications that retrieve, manage, or fulfill seller orders using the listed Get Order Detail and Get Order List endpoints are affected. Applications that parse the `orders[]` response or map `order_type` values should review these changes.
### What action is required?

1. Review the relevant Get Order Detail and Get Order List response schemas. Where these fields are consumed, update response parsers and data models to recognize the new boolean fields: `is_refundable_sample`, `authentication_required`, and `is_subscription_order`.
2. For Get Order Detail version `202507`, update `order_type` enum handling to recognize `AUCTION`.
3. Use `is_refundable_sample` to identify refundable sample orders, and use `is_subscription_order` to identify subscription orders where supported.
4. When `authentication_required` is `true`, authentication must be completed before shipment using an approved in-house authentication service.

## 2026-10-21 · Analytics · 【Important】Analytics API Changelog: Endpoint Retirements and Query Window Changes

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/8z0tllw9

TikTok Shop Open Platform will implement the following Analytics API changes on two effective dates. ISVs should review their integrations and complete the required updates before the applicable effective date.

* **November 6, 2026:** Maximum query window changes for two endpoints.
* **December 28, 2026:** Retirement of the single-video performance endpoint and older shop video list API versions.

### 1. Single-video performance endpoint retirement
**Effective date: December 28, 2026**
The following endpoint will be retired:
**API Name:** [Get Shop Video Performance Details](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-shop-video-performance-details-202509)
**Endpoint:** `GET /analytics/:version/shop_videos/{video_id}/performance`
**Required action:** Migrate to the batch performance endpoint:
**API Name:** [Get Shop Videos Performance Details](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-shop-videos-performance-details-202609)
**Endpoint:** `GET /analytics/:version/shop_videos/batch_performance`
Update your request parameters and response handling according to the batch endpoint documentation.
### 2. Retirement of older shop video list API versions
**Effective date: December 28, 2026**
Affected endpoint:
**API Name:** [Get Shop Video Performance List](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-shop-video-performance-list-202509)
**Endpoint:** `GET /analytics/:version/shop_videos/performance`
Versions **202409** and **202509** will be retired. Version **202605** will remain available.
**Required action:** Migrate integrations using either retiring version to:
**API Name:** [Get Shop Video Performance List](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-shop-video-performance-list-202605)
**Endpoint:** `GET /analytics/202605/shop_videos/performance`
### 3. Shop hourly performance: maximum 7 days per request
**Effective date: November 6, 2026**
Affected endpoint:
**API Name:** [Get Shop Performance Per Hour](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-shop-performance-per-hour-202510)
**Endpoint:** `GET /analytics/:version/shop/performance/:date/performance_per_hour`
The maximum query window will be **7 days per request**. The historical date range available for querying will remain unchanged.
If a request spans more than 7 days, the API will return data for the first 7 days by default, covering the requested start date and the following 6 days. For example, a request for **October 1 to October 30** will return data for **October 1 to October 7** by default.
**Required action:** To retrieve data for a longer period, split the date range into requests covering no more than 7 days each and combine the results.
### 4. Shop video list: maximum 30 days per request
**Effective date: November 6, 2026**
Affected endpoint:
**API Name:** [Get Shop Video Performance List](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-shop-video-performance-list-202605)
**Endpoint:** `GET /analytics/:version/shop_videos/performance`
The maximum query window will be **30 days per request**. The historical date range available for querying will remain unchanged.
**Required action:** To retrieve data for a longer period, split the date range into requests covering no more than 30 days each and combine the results.

## 2026-10-21 · Products · New API: Suggest Brands (202609) Is Now Available

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/xntktl90

The **Suggest Brands API** is now available on TikTok Shop Open Platform. ISVs can use product information to retrieve brand suggestions and incorporate them into their product listing workflows.
**Availability:** Available now
**API version:** 202609
**Endpoint:** `POST /product/202609/brands/suggest`
### What's new

* **Product-based brand suggestions:** Provide the required `product_title` to request suggested brands. You can also supply `category_id`, `description`, and `images` as optional inputs.
* **Brand details and authorization status:** The response lists brands in recommendation order. Each entry includes the brand's `id`, `name`, and `is_authorized` status for the shop.

### Integration notes

* If images are provided, the recommendation service currently uses only the first image.
* The `brands` list is empty when no suggestions are available. Handle this case in your brand-selection flow.

### Get started
Add brand suggestions to your listing workflow so sellers can review the returned options when selecting a product's brand.
See the [Suggest Brands API documentation](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/suggest-brands-202609) for authentication requirements, request and response schemas, and error handling.

## 2026-10-19 · Orders · For SEA L2L Market: Update on SLA fields for Order Fulfillment in Open API

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/vh1mgiuw

### Change Summary
From 15 October 2026, this update adds two new response fields to the Order Open API, covering both **getorderlist** and **getorderdetail**. The fields expose fast fulfillment SLA timestamps for shipping and collection actions, so sellers and partners can identify the required SLA time directly from order data.
| Field | Data type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `fast_rts_sla_time` | int | Indicates the fast fulfillment SLA for shipping action. The value is a Unix timestamp and applies to the SEA market only. |
| `fast_collection_sla_time` | int | Indicates the fast fulfillment SLA for collection action. The value is a Unix timestamp and applies to the SEA market only. |
### What is changing in detail?
Two new fields will be added under the response parameter path **Data > Orders** for both **getorderlist** and **getorderdetail**.
Use this field to ensure you can meet the Fast Delivery Rate (FDR) SLA to increase your FDR performance (applicable for SEA Local to Local market).
Shipping according to the FDR timeline helps merchants improve their FDR, which in turn **boosts your SES scores** and allows you to **obtain relevant benefits.** 
Learn more below :
- [Malaysia](https://seller-my.tiktok.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=3651539487557377&role=1&course_type=1&from=search)
- [Thailand](https://seller-th.tiktok.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=3470372616095489&role=1&course_type=1&from=search)
- [Vietnam](https://seller-vn.tiktok.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=3689018080577297&role=1&course_type=1&from=search)
- [Singapore](https://seller-sg.tiktok.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=3689018080495377&role=1&course_type=1&from=search)
- [Indonesia](https://seller-id.tokopedia.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=3448561695213313&role=1&course_type=&from=search)
- [Philippines](https://seller-ph.tiktok.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=3651539487819521&role=1&course_type=1&from=search)
### Which markets are affected?
This change applies to the **SEA market (Local to Local) only** . No impact is expected for markets outside SEA. 
### Who is affected?
This change affects sellers, partners, and internal systems that consume **getorderlist** or **getorderdetail** response data and need to read fast fulfillment SLA timestamps for order fulfillment, shipping, collection, or SLA monitoring scenarios.
### Which version is applicable?
Get Order List - 202309
Get Order Detail - 202309, 202507
### What action is required?
For API consumers: update response parsing logic to read `fast_rts_sla_time` and `fast_collection_sla_time` under **Data > Orders** when needed to map the two fields to the corresponding fulfillment SLA to ensure fulfillment within FDR SLA

## 2026-10-18 · Other · 2026 September TikTok Shop API Updates Summary

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/5daswqdc

#### Summary
Below is a summary of TikTok Shop Open API updates for **September 2026**. Three of these updates are **Breaking Changes**: the removal of two buyer-fault cancellation reasons from the Cancel Order API, the Shipping Template widget sunset (both effective **October 31**), and the retirement of the single-video Analytics details API (no later than **December 28**). New category attribute and compliance requirements also take effect in the UK, EU, US, and Malaysia **from late September**. Please assess the impact first, then complete the required migrations and field updates by each market's effective date.
This document uses two severity markers. 🔴 **[Breaking]** means your integration stops working unless you migrate. ⚠️ **[Action Required]** means the API stays compatible, but products, orders, labels, or events can fail if you do not adapt.
For the full record, see the Changelog linked at the end of this document.
#### 1. Breaking Changes and Key Updates
| Update | Required Actions | Key Date |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ⚠️ **[Action Required] Webhook Egress IP Update**  Webhook traffic from the **SG** and **EU** egress regions now comes from new IP ranges as well. US egress IPs are unchanged, and all previously published IPs remain valid. Payloads, event types, headers, signature verification, and retry behavior are unchanged. If your endpoint filters by source IP and you do nothing, your own firewall drops events from the new ranges, so you **miss events with no error returned**. | · If you allowlist source IPs, add every published range to your firewall, WAF, security group, load balancer, API gateway, and reverse proxy rules. Add CIDR blocks as CIDR, not as single addresses.  · Allowlist every region you serve.  · After the update, check that events are arriving and review firewall drop logs.  · Use signature verification as the main authenticity check, with IP allowlisting as an extra layer. | **Update allowlists now** (see the notice for the latest effective date) |
| ⚠️ **[Action Required] US Combined Listing: Merge into Variants**  Sellers can merge separately listed products of the same style into one main product detail page. Merged child products get **new SKU IDs** under the main product. Their original child SKU IDs are returned through the existing virtual bundle structure: `data.skus[].combined_skus[].sku_id` in Get Product, and `line_items[].combined_listing_skus[].sku_id` in Get Order Detail. | · Use Get Product to keep main-to-child SKU mappings in sync.  · For fulfillment, read `combined_listing_skus` (`product_id`, `sku_id`, `seller_sku`, `sku_count`) in Get Order Detail.  · **If your integration uses only the order line's**`sku_id`**to find the original child SKU, you need to adapt it.**  · Price and inventory sync from the original child SKUs. Update those child products, not the main listing. | **Live** for US sellers with the feature enabled |
| ⚠️ **[Action Required] UK & EU Dangerous Goods (DG) Compliance Attributes**  For selected UK and EU categories, Get Attributes returns DG attributes as mandatory. Depending on the category, these include battery type and characteristics, packaging method, rechargeable status, aerosol and flammable liquid volume, SDS / UN38.3 documentation, and hazardous material classification. New listings without these values cannot be published. | · Read the DG attributes that Get Attributes returns for each category, and show them as mandatory in your listing flows.  · Collect and validate the values before submission.  · Pass DG values every time an affected listing is created or edited.  · Applies to local and cross-border sellers in the affected categories. | **Mandatory from September 22, 2026** |
| ⚠️ **[Action Required] US Category Attribute Updates: Auto Parts, Tires, Motorcycles, and Engine Oils**  Adds vehicle fitment, specification, and compliance attributes, such as year / make / model / engine, MPN, OEM part number, EPA numbers, and CARB compliance status. All 10 tire categories get mandatory attributes, including Installation Recommendation, For Highway Use, Tire Class, size / load / speed specifications, Tire Identification Number, EPA establishment / registration numbers (`102692` / `102694`), and CARB Compliance (`102697`). UTQG ratings are added to Passenger Car, Motorbike, and Lorry & SUV. | · Once the update takes effect, call Get Attributes for each affected leaf category, then refresh cached attributes and value mappings.  · Submit values through `product_attributes`, following the mandatory and conditional rules the API returns.  · Check listing flows for missing mandatory attributes, predefined values, and custom input. | **Planned for September 30, 2026** |
| ⚠️ **[Action Required] Malaysia: Key Attributes Become Mandatory for Selected Hi-Po Categories**  Selected attributes change from optional to required in Health (15 L3 categories, 4 attributes), Sports & Outdoor (4 L3 categories, 4 attributes), and Tools & Hardware (40 L3 categories, Warranty Duration / Warranty Type). A new structured attribute, **Energy Efficiency Star Rating**, is required for 8 Large Home Appliances L3 categories. | · Review MY product creation and update flows for the affected categories.  · Fill in every newly required attribute before submission.  · Add support for Energy Efficiency Star Rating.  · This is a category configuration rollout. There is no new API version. | **Effective September 30, 2026** |
| ⚠️ **[Action Required] US Shop Performance Score (SPS) Update**  The new SPS replaces the existing page in Seller Center, with revised Product Satisfaction and Logistics & Fulfillment metrics. Customer Service is unchanged. Existing SPS APIs stay available, but their scores and scoring rules become **reference only**. New SPS APIs will be announced separately. | · If you still display data from existing SPS APIs, label it "Previous SPS version — for reference only".  · Review alerts and recommendations built on the previous scoring rules.  · Send sellers to Seller Center for current scores and improvement guidance. | **October 15, 2026** |
| 🔴 **[Breaking] Cancel Order API: Two Buyer-Fault Cancellation Reasons Removed**  In Cancel Order (`POST /return_refund/202602/cancellations`), the `cancel_reason` values `seller_cancel_paid_reason_address_not_deliver` and `seller_cancel_paid_reason_buyer_requested_cancellation` will be rejected. This matches Seller Center behavior. `seller_cancel_reason_unable_to_fulfill_order` is available as the alternative. Applies to standard and auction orders, for local and cross-border sellers. | · Remove both reasons from every cancellation reason picker, mapping table, default, and fallback.  · Re-map affected cases to `out_of_stock`, `wrong_price`, or `unable_to_fulfill_order`.  · When you get `25001020` / `25001015` / `25001014`, ask the seller to pick another reason. Do not retry the same payload.  · Make the same change if you still call earlier Cancel Order versions. | **October 31, 2026** |
| 🔴 **[Breaking] Shipping Template Widget Sunset**  The 2024 legacy Shipping template widget in Partner Center is being retired, and any slot that still embeds it will fail to load. There is **no replacement widget or API**. Shipping template configuration moves entirely to Seller Center. Other widgets, including Shipping by TikTok, are not affected, and no re-authorization is needed. | · List every place in your app that embeds the widget.  · Replace each one with a redirect to the Seller Center shipping template page.  · Update onboarding and help copy. Ask sellers to review any templates they created through the widget.  · Release the updated app version before the sunset date. | **October 31, 2026** |
| ⚠️ **[Action Required] GS POP Sellers Shipping to the EU: Product Identifier (PID) Requirement**  Goods shipped from outside the EU directly to EU consumers need a valid PID for customs declaration. The platform provides the M-PID. Sellers must provide **at least one** of Seller SKU (NS-PID) or Product Identifier (S-PID, such as GTIN / EAN / UPC / JAN / ISBN). | · Make sure at least one of Seller SKU or Product Identifier has a valid value.  · NS-PID format: letters and numbers only, 6–50 characters, with no spaces or special characters.  · New products must follow the rule from September 1. Fix existing products before the end of October.  · Update validation and sync logic in listing tools and ERP integrations. | **New products: from September 1, 2026**  **Existing products: by end of October 2026**  **Customs enforcement: November 1, 2026** |
| ⚠️ **[Action Required] US Wig Material Becomes Mandatory, with Conditional Certification**  The Wig material attribute (`102428`) becomes mandatory for 15 US wig categories. "Human hair" requires the human hair percentage attribute. "100% human hair" requires a human hair content test report submitted through `certifications`. "Synthetic hair" requires nothing extra under this rule. | · Refresh Get Attributes (`requirement_conditions`) and Get Category Rules (`product_certifications`).  · Make Wig material a mandatory single-select field in your listing forms.  · Upload the report with Upload Product File or Upload Product Image, then pass the result in `certifications`.  · Do not set a material value without the seller's confirmation. Test with Check Product Listing. | **Rollout started September 21, 2026. API enforcement starts November 21, 2026** |
| 🔴 **[Breaking] Analytics: Single-Video Details API Retirement**  Get Shop Videos Performance Details (`GET /analytics/202609/shop_videos/batch_performance`) replaces Get Shop Video Performance Details (`GET /analytics/202509/shop_videos/{video_id}/performance`). The 202609 versions of Shop Performance, Video Overview, LIVE Overview, and LIVE List also remove fields and change response structures. See section 2.1. | · Move single-video calls to the batch API and handle errors for each video separately.  · When you upgrade to 202609, map fields by metric definition, not by field name.  · Query product-level details through Get Shop Video Product Performance List. | **Old API retired no later than December 28, 2026** |
#### 2. Featured New Capabilities ( Recommended)
This month the platform introduced three capability sets: **deeper shop, video, and LIVE analytics**, **real-time inventory change notifications**, and **LIVE auction support across product, promotion, and content APIs**. They help partners replace inferred or manual work with reliable, programmable data flows. We recommend reviewing them first.
##### 2.1 Analytics API Upgrade: 8 APIs Enhanced, Including a New Batch Video Details API
Analytics API coverage expands across shop sales and channel attribution, video sales and engagement, and LIVE sales and viewing performance.

* **Shop sales and channel attribution**: `Get Shop Performance` (202609) adds GMV breakdowns by LIVE, video, and product card, and by seller and creator content, Shop Tab, and search. It also adds average order value, paying customers, cancellations and returns, shipping fees, taxes, and product impressions and clicks.
* **Hourly tracking**: `Get Shop Performance Per Hour` (202510) adds order, customer, content-attributed GMV, and product traffic metrics to both hourly data and daily totals.
* **Video performance**: `Get Shop Video Performance Overview` (202609) adds video-attributed GMV, conversion, views, and GPM, plus a new `today` flag for real-time data. `Get Shop Video Performance List` (202605) adds attributed GMV, engagement, video-to-LIVE clicks, completion rate, and creator information.
* **Batch video details (new)**: `Get Shop Videos Performance Details` (202609) queries up to **500**`video_ids` in one call. It returns sales, engagement, audience profiles, and video metadata, with errors reported per video.
* **Video product efficiency**: `Get Shop Video Product Performance List` (202509) adds purchasing customers, product CTR, GMV per 1,000 product impressions, and product impressions and clicks.
* **LIVE and auction performance**: `Get Shop LIVE Performance Overview` and `Get Shop LIVE Performance List` (202609) add LIVE- and auction-attributed sales, direct and indirect GMV, viewing and engagement rates, a new `traffic` structure, and the data update time.

**Integration notes**: Public by default. Metrics added to existing versions (202510 / 202605 / 202509) are additive. The 202609 versions include field removals and structure changes. For example, `sales.gmv` is flattened to `amount` / `currency`. LIVE `account_type` values change, and the LIVE List requires `account_type`. LIVE `avg_viewing_duration` changes from String to Int64. Map fields by metric definition, not by name. Do not add up GMV metrics that use different attribution definitions. Treat values that exist only in offline data differently from a value of 0.
**Recommended for**: Seller development teams and analytics ISVs that build shop, content, or LIVE performance reports.
##### 2.2 Inventory Changed by Shop Webhook: Real-Time SKU Inventory Sync
The new `Inventory Changed by Shop` webhook (`type: 71`) notifies you whenever a SKU's inventory quantity changes. It covers changes from order creation, cancellation, and shipment, manual Seller Center adjustments, OpenAPI updates, inventory locked or released for campaigns and creator collaborations, and automatic system replenishment.

* **Post-change snapshot**: `quantity_snapshot_after_change` returns total, available, committed, and in-shop inventory, plus inventory locked for campaigns and creators.
* **Per-change details**: the `change_detail` array returns the trigger source, time, and quantity change for each inventory category.
* **Built-in idempotency**: `event_id` deduplicates whole events, and `idempotency_key` deduplicates individual changes.

**Integration notes**: Available in all markets. This is a new webhook event, not a new API version, and it is delivered to your configured webhook URL. A snapshot is the inventory after the change, and a delta is an increase or decrease. Never apply both to the same balance.
**Recommended for**: ERP, inventory management, and multichannel commerce integrations that need accurate, real-time stock levels.
##### 2.3 LIVE Auction Support Across Product, Promotion, and Content APIs
Three related updates let partners manage auction products from end to end:

* **Product selling format**: Create Product, Edit Product (202309 / 202509), and Check Product Listing accept the optional `selling_format` field: `FIXED_PRICE` (default) or `FIXED_PRICE_AND_LIVE_AUCTION`, which requires a starting bid price. Get Product and Search Products return `selling_format`, including the response-only value `LIVE_AUCTION`. Never send that value back in a request.
* **Promotion eligibility**: Create / Update Promotion Activity (202309) accept the optional `auction_support_type` (`ALL` by default, or `EXCLUDE_AUCTION`). Get Activity and Search Promotion Activities return it. Available now in all markets.
* **Content restriction**: Post Shoppable Photos and Post Shoppable Video reject products sold by auction only, with error code `170001063`. Ask users to replace the product before retrying.

**Integration notes**: All changes are backward compatible. They add only optional request fields, response fields, enum values, and one new validation error. No new permission scope is introduced.
**Recommended for**: Listing tools, promotion management apps, and content or LIVE commerce partners that work with auction sellers.
#### 3. Other Updates
| API Category | Update Summary | Details |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Fulfillment API | UK & EU: Create Packages supports seller-edited parcel dimensions and weight | Create Packages (`/fulfillment/202512/packages`), previously used only in the US, now supports Shipped by Platform (4PL) label creation in the UK and EU. Pass `line_items[].dimension` (length, width, and height together, in `CM` for EU) and `line_items[].weight` (in `GRAM` for EU). The platform uses the latest values for routing and label purchase. This reduces shipping fee deviations, seller disputes, and carrier chargebacks. The flow is optional: you can pass product-level dimensions directly or add a seller review step before label creation. |
| Finance API | 8 new fee and tax properties for Growth Packages and auto-post shoppable video | Get Transactions by Order and Get Transactions by Statement (202501), and Get Unsettled Transactions (202507), add the following.  Under `fee_tax_breakdown.fee`: `new_customer_growth_package_fee`, `store_gmv_growth_package_fee`, `target_product_gmv_growth_package_fee`, `auto_post_shoppable_video_commission_fee`.  Under `fee_tax_breakdown.tax`: `new_customer_growth_package_fee_tax`, `store_gmv_growth_package_fee_sales_tax`, `target_product_gmv_growth_package_tax`, `auto_post_shoppable_video_sales_tax`.  Growth Package fee fields apply in US / JP / EU / UK. Tax fields are filled for the US only and return `"0"` elsewhere. The change is response-only and non-breaking, with no new scope. |
| Product API (Opportunity) | Get Opportunity Detail and List Opportunity add tag codes, rewards, and reference product fields | Both APIs add `tag_codes` (`List<String>`, matched one-to-one and in order with `tags`), `external_product_id`, and `reference_source`. The last two are LATAM only, and `reference_source` is returned only when `external_product_id` is present. Get Opportunity Detail also adds `tags`. List Opportunity adds `reward_info` and an optional `tag_codes` request filter, for example `["CORE_PRODUCT", "TOP_PRODUCT"]`. Update your response models, and handle responses where `reference_source` is missing. |
#### References

* Changelog: [https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/changelog](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/changelog)
* API Documentation: [https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/)

If you have questions about these updates or need support, please [submit a ticket](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/kwfskwpj) to contact us. Thank you.

## 2026-10-09 · Affiliate · For All Market：Creator Outreach Quota Rule Adjustment

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/vzfn7sdc

#### 1. Background
Targeted Collaboration is being upgraded so that invitation capacity follows **fit** rather than volume. Today a seller has one flat weekly outreach budget that can be spent on any creator. After the upgrade, capacity is allocated by **seller tier × creator level**, and sellers whose invitations actually convert into posts earn bonus capacity for higher-level creators.
##### 1.1 Old model vs new model
| Dimension | Before | After (from Oct 15, 2026; subject to actual effective date) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Quota granularity | One shared weekly pool per shop | **One pool per creator level**, on top of the shop's total |
| What limits a creator | Follower cap | Creator level, plus the remaining quota for that level |
| Extra capacity | Aggregate bonus quota | Bonus quota **per level**, driven by exposure-to-post performance, **consumed first** |
| Failure mode | Entire batch fails when the pool is empty | Only the exhausted level fails, returning **16024049** |
| Pre-check | Remaining total = `quota_sum - quota_use` | Per-level remaining, plus a per-creator **eligibility check** |

#### 2. API changes
**Compatibility**: no existing field, enum, or response shape is removed, and **no new permission scope** is introduced — already-authorized apps do not need re-authorization. The behavioral break is on the **invitation result**, not on the request contract: a request that was accepted before can now be rejected with `16024049`.

##### 2.1 Creator discovery gains creator_level
| API | Change | Why it matters |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Seller Search Creator on Marketplace**  `POST /affiliate_seller/{202608\|202508}/marketplace_creators/search` | New request parameter `creator_level` (`int64`) — filter candidates by creator level.  New response field `data.creators[].creator_level` (`int64`). | Candidate lists can be pulled level by level so a batch never mixes levels whose quotas differ. |
| **Get Marketplace Creator Performance**  `GET /affiliate_seller/{202608\|202508}/marketplace_creators/{creator_user_id}` | New response field `data.creator.creator_level` (`int64`). | Creator detail pages and CRM records can show the level that decides which quota bucket an invitation will hit. |
##### 2.2 Quota API returns per-level detail
**Get Weekly Creator Outreach Quota** — `GET /affiliate_seller/202607/creator_outreach/quota`. All existing fields remain unchanged. A new `quota_detail` array is added to the response (if the shop has not been included in the rollout, this field returns empty).
| New field | Type | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `quota_detail` | array<object> | — | Quota breakdown by creator level group. |
| `quota_detail.creator_levels` | list | `[1,2,3]` | Creator levels this entry covers. |
| `quota_detail.quota_period_sum` | int32 | 300 | Standard quota granted for the current period. |
| `quota_detail.quota_period_use` | int32 | 100 | Standard quota already used. |
| `quota_detail.quota_bonus_sum` | int32 | 100 | Bonus quota granted for the current period. |
| `quota_detail.quota_bonus_use` | int32 | 100 | Bonus quota already used. |

**Remaining capacity for a level group** = `(quota_bonus_sum - quota_bonus_use) + (quota_period_sum - quota_period_use)`. Bonus is consumed first, so tools that only track the standard numbers will under-count what the seller can still send.
##### 2.3 New API: Check Creator Outreach Eligibility
A bulk pre-check for up to 100 candidates per call — determines whether each creator can be successfully invited and whether re-inviting is needed.
###### API path
| Item | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Method / path | `POST /affiliate/{version}/creator_outreach/eligibility/check` |
###### Request parameters
| Field | Type | Required | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `creator_ids` | array<string> | Yes | List of candidate creator IDs. Recommended: 1–100 per call. |
###### Response parameters
| Field | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `code` | integer | OpenAPI response status code. |
| `message` | string | Response message. |
| `request_id` | string | Request trace ID. |
| `data` | object | Business response payload. |
| `data.creator_quota_infos[]` | array<object> | Per-creator eligibility check results. |
| ` .creator_id` | string | Creator ID. |
| ` .is_connect` | boolean | Whether the creator is already connected with the seller. |
| ` .is_paired` | boolean | Whether the creator is already paired with the seller. |
| ` .is_blocked` | boolean | Whether the seller and creator have a mutual block. |
| ` .block_types` | array<string> | Block types, if applicable. |
| ` .is_conform_to_threshold` | boolean | Whether the creator meets the seller's outreach threshold. |
| Response flag | How to use it |
| --- | --- |
| `is_blocked` / `block_types` | Drop the creator from the batch. Surface the reason in the UI instead of letting the invitation fail. |
| `is_connect` / `is_paired` | Already connected or paired — route to the existing conversation rather than spending new quota. |
| `is_conform_to_threshold` | **The key gate for this launch**: `false` means the creator sits above what this seller's tier can reach, so an invitation would be rejected. |
##### 2.4 New error: quota exhausted for a creator level
| Code | Message | When it is returned |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **16024049** | `Outreach creator level has exceeded the quota limit.` | Invitation creation (**Create Target Collaboration**) when the target creator's level has no remaining standard or bonus quota. |

**Do not blind-retry** **`16024049`**. Quota does not come back until the next Sunday refresh. Retrying burns rate limit and, in queue-based tools, can stall the rest of the batch. Re-read the quota, drop that level from the current run, and keep sending levels that still have capacity.

#### 3. Recommended integration flow
The shape of the change: quota checking moves from **one number before the batch** to **per-level remaining + per-creator eligibility check**.

![Image](https://p16-arcosite-sg.ibyteimg.com/tos-alisg-i-k9wyc2ijk0-sg/054ee6af15ee400984c5217b4c27fb6d~tplv-k9wyc2ijk0-image.image)

##### 3.1 Step by step

1. **Read the quota first.** Call **Get Weekly Creator Outreach Quota** once per planning run and cache `quota_detail` per level group together with `start_time` / `end_time`. Treat `unlimited=true` as "no per-level cap", but re-check it every period — allowlisted shops are moving off it.
2. **Pull candidates by level.** Use the new `creator_level` filter in **Seller Search Creator on Marketplace** and keep `creator_level` on your candidate records. Do not build a batch that mixes levels before the quota is applied.
3. **Bulk eligibility check.** Send up to 100 `creator_ids` to **Check Creator Outreach Eligibility** and remove anyone with `is_blocked=true`, `is_conform_to_threshold=false`, or an existing relationship you do not want to re-invite.
4. **Bucket and cap.** Group the survivors by `creator_level`, then truncate each bucket to that level's remaining capacity (bonus first, then standard). Also apply the unchanged daily cap of 200 creators.
5. **Send invitations by bucket.** Call **Create Target Collaboration** per bucket so that one exhausted level cannot fail an otherwise valid batch.
6. **Handle** **`16024049` as a stop signal for that level.** Refresh the quota, mark the level as exhausted until the next Sunday, and continue with the remaining buckets.
7. **Close the loop.** Use **Get Weekly Creator Outreach Records** (`has_sent_im_message` → `has_sent_invitation` → `is_paired`) to de-duplicate the next run and to report which levels actually convert. Bonus quota is earned from exposure-to-post performance, so wasting high-level quota on poor-fit creators reduces the seller's future capacity.

##### 3.2 What to change in your product surface
| Surface | Recommended change |
| --- | --- |
| Quota widget | Replace the single "remaining invitations" number with a per-level breakdown, and show bonus separately from standard so sellers understand why one level runs out first. |
| Creator list | Show `creator_level` as a column or tag, add a level filter, and offer an "invitable now" toggle driven by remaining quota plus the eligibility check. |
| Batch builder | Warn before submission when a selection exceeds a level's remaining quota, and let the seller trim the selection instead of discovering it through errors. |
| Error messaging | Map `16024049` to a seller-readable message: this level's weekly quota is used up, new quota arrives Sunday, other levels are still available. |
| Scheduling | Anchor recurring outreach jobs to the Sunday refresh and spread sends across the 7-day window rather than front-loading Monday. |

#### 4. Timeline and action items
| Date | What happens |
| --- | --- |
| Before Oct 15, 2026 | New response fields and the eligibility check API are available for integration and testing. |
| **Oct 15, 2026** | Gradual rollout begins in the **US** market. Some shops see tiered quota first — your integration must handle both the old aggregate behavior and the new per-level behavior in the same release. |
| After rollout | Coverage expands. Allowlisted shops move to standard tiered quota as their review completes. |

**ISV action checklist**

- [ ] Read and store per-level `quota_detail`, including bonus quota.
- [ ] Persist `creator_level` on creator records from search and creator detail.
- [ ] Call the eligibility check before every invitation batch.
- [ ] Bucket invitations by creator level and cap each bucket by remaining quota.
- [ ] Handle `16024049` without retry loops, and expose it to sellers in plain language.

#### 5. FAQ
**Does this introduce a new permission scope?**
No. Existing Affiliate seller scopes are reused, and authorized apps do not need re-authorization.
**Do API invitations get a separate quota?**
No. API, third-party tools, and Seller Center all draw on the same weekly quota for the shop.
**Will old integrations stop working?**
Requests keep the same shape and existing fields keep the same meaning. What changes is the outcome: invitations targeting a level with no remaining quota are rejected with `16024049` instead of succeeding.
**How does a seller get more capacity for high-level creators?**
By performance. Creators who see an invitation and go on to post drive the bonus quota for higher levels, and bonus quota is consumed before standard quota.
**Does platform-led creator matching count against quota?**
No, so matched collaborations should not be counted against the numbers returned by the quota API.

## 2026-10-09 · Other · Buyer Personal Information Masking

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/pgd9hfyy

**Developer Notice**: TikTok Shop will control access to unmasked buyer personal information based on the app category; apps without unmasked-data access will receive masked values, so please review your app category and data-processing logic in advance.

#### 1. What Is Changing
Starting from October 12, To reduce unnecessary exposure of buyer personal information, TikTok Shop will determine whether relevant APIs return unmasked or masked buyer information based on the shop market and App Category.
The controlled information includes the buyer's name, phone number, email address, and shipping address*;
> * Tax information is also included for Brazil market.

#### 2. APIs Affected

* Order: [Get Order Detail](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-order-detail-202507) and [Get Order List](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-order-list-202309)
* Fulfillment: [Get Package Detail](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-package-detail-202309)

Non-sensitive fields are not affected; sensitive fields will be returned as unmasked or masked values according to the app-category rules.
#### 3. App Category Rules
The following App Categories may receive unmasked buyer personal information under platform rules because they require such information for order processing or fulfillment:
| App Category | Typical Use Case |
| --- | --- |
| Connectors | Connecting merchant systems with e-commerce platforms for order or fulfillment processing |
| Enterprise Resource Planning | ERP-based order and fulfillment management |
| Multi-Channel Management | Multi-channel order and inventory management |
| Print On Demand | On-demand production and fulfillment |
| Order Management (OMS/WMS) | Order, warehouse, and shipping management |
| Shipping | Logistics, shipping-label, and delivery services |
| TikTok Shop Seller | Merchant-operated order and fulfillment processing |
| Dropshipping | Dropshipping fulfillment |
| Loyalty & Rewards | Customer loyalty and rewards services |
| CRM | Customer relationship management |
| Semi Consignment | Semi-consignment fulfillment |

Other App Categories will receive masked data by default. Apps must select the category that accurately reflects their actual capabilities, and TikTok Shop may review access to unmasked information based on the app's functions and data-use purpose.
If an app's category returns masked data by default but the app provides order fulfillment or third-party logistics (3PL) services, the developer may contact TikTok Shop Customer Support to apply for an App Key allowlist.
#### FAQ
##### Which fields can my app receive in unmasked form?
The fields depend on the market, App Category, and App Key permissions; sensitive fields without unmasked-data access will be returned as masked values.
##### Our category is not on the list, but we need buyer information to fulfill orders. What should we do?
Contact TikTok Shop Customer Support to apply for the allowlist and prepare materials that demonstrate a genuine fulfillment need.
##### Will non-sensitive fields also be masked?
No. This control applies only to fields classified as sensitive information.

If you encounter API response or permission issues, contact us through the Partner Center Customer Support channel.

## 2026-10-08 · Fulfillment · For EU Markets: Removal of approved couriers for Shipped by Seller (3PL) domestic orders

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/7oobdgft

### What is changing?
TikTok Shop periodically reviews the couriers approved for the **Shipped by Seller (3PL)** fulfillment method.
Effective **October 14, 2026**, the couriers listed below will be removed from the approved courier list for **domestic EU orders**. This change applies to **local-to-local (L2L) sellers only**.
| EU (L2L ONLY) | Provider name | Provider ID |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ES | SEUR | 7038836933132289793 |
| ES | Ontime | 7447770919805650695 |
| ES | Sending | 7038808610033370882 |
| ES | Zeleris | 7203274924226512642 |
| ES | Redur | 7563974390129968914 |
| ES | Dachser | 7038795162650150658 |
| IT | TNT | 7073775627609835266 |
| IT | TNT Italy | 7038838469963024129 |
| IT | Arco Spedizzione | 7641130961962452756 |
| IT | TWS | 7615212966140774151 |
| IT | Fercam | 7533260542899652372 |
| FR | Geodis | 7359034210140555014 |
| FR | Relais Colis | 7470765163365271304 |
| FR | Vente-unique Delivery | 7642687585852065556 |
| FR | heppner FR | 7643733649539991303 |
| DE | GEL | 7533260804896851719 |
| DE | TNT | 7073775627609835266 |
| DE | Kuehne + Nagel | 7572505951475861256 |
### Which markets are affected?
This change affects domestic orders in **Spain (ES), Italy (IT), France (FR), and Germany (DE)**. The scope is **L2L only**.
### Who is affected?
This change affects developers and applications that support sellers using **Shipped by Seller (3PL)** for domestic orders in ES, IT, FR, and DE, where those integrations use or maintain approved courier selections for L2L workflows.
### What action is required?

1. **Update your applications.** Use the most current shipping provider IDs returned by the [Get Shipping Providers endpoint](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-shipping-providers-202309). ***<u>This endpoint should be called dynamically as some sellers may be whitelisted and still have access to removed provider ids.***</u>
2. **Inform your seller base.** Notify sellers of this change and the available options below to help them avoid order sync issues.
3. **Ask sellers to choose one of the following options:**
   * **Option A — Switch to an approved courier.** Sellers must switch to an approved courier before **October 14, 2026** to avoid order cancellations. See the [approved courier list](https://seller-ie.tiktok.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=3317258248472321&identity=1).
   * **Option B — Switch to Shipped via Platform.** TikTok provides shipping labels in Seller Center and updates tracking information automatically. Read the [Shipped via Platform guidance](https://seller-es.tiktok.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=3148241730815745&role=1&course_type=1&from=search&identity=1&account_ts=1754407463061&register_libra={).
   * **Option C — Switch to Fulfilled by TikTok.** TikTok stores, packs, and ships sellers’ orders.
4. **No action is required for existing tracking numbers.** Tracking numbers updated before **October 14, 2026** will continue to be tracked normally after that date.

## 2026-09-28 · Analytics · Analytics API Changelog: Enhanced Shop, Video, and LIVE Analytics

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/6az7iwe9

**Release date: 2026-09-28 | Availability: Public by default | API documentation: Publicly visible**
This update expands Analytics API coverage for shop sales and channel attribution, video sales and engagement, and LIVE sales and viewing performance. New attributed GMV, traffic conversion, and LIVE auction metrics help developers analyze shop and content performance in greater detail.
**Video details API migration:** The new `Get Shop Videos Performance Details` replaces `Get Shop Video Performance Details`. The previous API will be retired within three months of this release (no later than 2026-12-28). Migrate as soon as possible.

Seller development teams and analytics ISVs can use these updates in their own systems to compare sales contributions from seller and creator content, LIVE streams, videos, and product cards, track hourly changes in shop performance, and assess impressions, clicks, sales, and engagement across videos and LIVE streams.
### API Update Overview
| API | Version | Key Updates |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Get Shop Performance | 202609 | Adds GMV source breakdowns, average order value, paying customers, cancellations and returns, shipping fees, taxes, and product click and impression metrics. |
| Get Shop Performance Per Hour | 202510 | Adds order, customer, content-attributed GMV, and product traffic metrics to both hourly data and daily totals. |
| Get Shop Video Performance Overview | 202609 | Adds video-attributed sales, daily average customers, conversion, view, and product-viewer metrics. Some metrics are available only for offline data. |
| Get Shop Video Performance List | 202605 | Adds attributed GMV, engagement, product traffic, video-to-LIVE clicks, completion rate, and creator information. |
| Get Shop Videos Performance Details | 202609 | New batch video details （Max 500） API returning sales, engagement, audience profiles, metadata, and errors for each video. Replaces Get Shop Video Performance Details, which will be retired within three months of this release. |
| Get Shop Video Product Performance List | 202509 | Adds product click-through rate, purchasing customers, GMV per 1,000 product impressions, and product clicks and impressions. |
| Get Shop LIVE Performance Overview | 202609 | Adds LIVE and auction-attributed sales, direct and indirect GMV, viewing and conversion metrics, and the data update time. |
| Get Shop LIVE Performance List | 202609 | Adds creator, sales, auction, engagement-rate, and traffic metrics for each LIVE session, with account-filter and response-structure changes. |

### What's Updated
#### 1. Shop Sales and Channel Attribution
**Get Shop Performance — Version 202609**
Expanded shop sales and traffic metrics include average order value, average SKU order value, paying customers, counts of cancellations and returns, shipping fees, taxes, tax-inclusive GMV, and GMV including TikTok co-funding. More detailed attribution data for LIVE, videos, and product cards adds breakdowns by seller and creator content, Shop Tab, search, and other sources. Product impressions, clicks, and their unique counts help identify the sources of changes in shop sales.
**Get Shop Performance Per Hour — Additional Metrics in Version 202510**
Both daily totals and hourly data now include average order value, average SKU order value, paying customers, order and SKU order metrics, GMV attributed to LIVE, videos, and product cards, and product impressions, clicks, and their unique counts. These additions support hourly analysis of sales and traffic alongside the day's overall performance.
#### 2. Video Sales and Content Performance
**Get Shop Video Performance Overview — Version 202609**
More detailed metrics cover video-attributed GMV, video GMV, indirect GMV, and attributed SKU orders. Additional metrics include daily average customers, click-to-SKU-order conversion rate, the ratio of product clicks to video views, GMV per 1,000 video views, video views, and product viewers. A new response field, `today`, indicates whether the interval contains real-time data for the current day, helping developers assess the sales contribution of video content at an aggregate level.
**Get Shop Video Performance List — Additional Metrics in Version 202605**
The video list adds total attributed GMV, indirect attributed GMV, click-to-order conversion rate, likes, comments, shares, new followers, and product impressions and clicks. It also adds video-to-LIVE clicks and click rate, video completion rate, an advertising eligibility flag, and creator information. The existing `gmv` field description now explicitly refers to GMV directly attributed to the video, supporting comparisons of sales, engagement, and traffic driven to LIVE streams across videos.
#### 3. Video Details and Associated Product Analysis
**Get Shop Videos Performance Details — New API, Version 202609**
Supports batch queries for video performance using `video_ids`. Returns sales data, engagement data, and audience profiles for the requested date range, aggregate or daily granularity, and currency, together with video information such as title, posting time, duration, thumbnail, and playback URL. The response can return errors for individual videos, allowing each result to be handled separately and content information to be connected with business performance in the same analysis view.
**New endpoint:** `GET /analytics/202609/shop_videos/batch_performance`
**Replaced endpoint:** `GET /analytics/202509/shop_videos/{video_id}/performance`
**Get Shop Video Product Performance List — Additional Metrics in Version 202509**
Building on existing GMV, units sold, and daily average buyers, the list of products associated with a video adds purchasing customers, product click-through rate, GMV per 1,000 product impressions, and product impressions and clicks. These metrics support comparisons of traffic and sales efficiency across products in the same video.
#### 4. LIVE Sales, Viewing Performance, and Engagement
**Get Shop LIVE Performance Overview — Version 202609**
More detailed metrics cover GMV, items sold, and SKU orders attributed to LIVE, along with direct and indirect LIVE GMV. The update also adds GMV, items sold, and SKU orders attributed to LIVE auction products, plus their average selling price. New metrics include views, average viewing duration, product click-through rate, the ratio of attributed SKU orders to product clicks, LIVE-attributed GMV per 1,000 impressions, and the data update time, supporting analysis of overall LIVE sales and viewing performance.
**Get Shop LIVE Performance List — Version 202609**
Expanded metrics for individual LIVE sessions cover creator information, attributed sales, orders, payment rate, average order value, and auction performance. A new, separate `traffic` structure organizes impressions, views, product clicks, conversion metrics, LIVE-attributed GMV per hour, and LIVE-attributed GMV per 1,000 impressions. Engagement rates supplement the existing counts of comments, follows, likes, and shares, and the data update time is also added, helping developers compare sales, viewing, and engagement performance across LIVE sessions.
### Developer Integration Notes
#### Plan Updates by API Version
**Version 202609:** Shop performance, video performance overview, LIVE performance overview, and the LIVE performance list include field removals or response structure changes. Review request parameters, field hierarchy, names, types, and metric definitions. The new Get Shop Videos Performance Details replaces the previous single-video details API and requires migration to batch requests and responses. The previous API will be retired within three months of this release.
**Additional metrics in existing versions:** Hourly shop performance uses 202510, the video performance list uses 202605, and the video product performance list uses 202509. Integrators can extend their data models and reports to incorporate the new metrics.
#### Update Request Parameters and Response Parsing

* **LIVE account filtering:** In the LIVE overview, `account_type` removes `OFFICIAL_ACCOUNTS` and `MARKETING_ACCOUNTS`, adds `LINKED_ACCOUNTS`, and retains `ALL` and `AFFILIATE_ACCOUNTS`. In the LIVE list, `account_type` becomes required and `ALL` is removed. Adapt each API separately.
* **Shop GMV structure:**`sales.gmv` now returns `amount` and `currency` directly. The previous `gmv.overall`, `gmv.breakdowns`, `sales.gross_revenue`, and `sales.avg_customers_count` are removed.
* **LIVE list parsing:** Several traffic metrics move from `interaction_performance` to `traffic`; `products_added` moves from `sales_performance` to `traffic`; and average viewing duration, `avg_viewing_duration`, changes from String to Int64, with the unit remaining seconds. The LIVE entry's `username` is removed, while the new creator structure provides `creator.user_name`.
* **Previous sales and conversion fields:** The LIVE overview removes `gmv`, `sku_orders`, `customers`, `items_sold`, `click_to_order_rate`, and `click_through_rate`; the video overview removes `gmv`, `click_through_rate`, `sku_orders`, and `avg_customers`. The LIVE list also removes some previous sales fields, including `gmv`, `different_products_sold`, `sku_orders`, `items_sold`, `customers`, `click_to_order_rate`, and `24h_live_gmv`. Update mappings based on the new metric definitions rather than replacing fields by name alone.

#### Review Metric Definitions and Data Availability

* **Current-day and historical data:** In the video overview, `gpm`, `shoppable_video_attributed_gmv`, `video_views`, and `video_product_viewers` are returned only for offline data. Distinguish an unavailable value from a value of 0 in your integration.
* **Click-through rate and GPM:** The video overview's `ctr` and the video list's `click_through_rate` use video views as the denominator. The video product list's and batch video details' `ctr`, as well as LIVE's `live_ctr`, use product impressions as the denominator. GPM in the video overview and video list is calculated per 1,000 video views; GPM in the video product list is calculated per 1,000 product impressions; and LIVE `show_gpm` is calculated per 1,000 LIVE impressions.
* **Amounts, ratios, and update times:** Read monetary values from `amount` together with its sibling `currency`. Convert ratios returned as raw decimals for display; for example, `0.0528` represents 5.28%. 
* **Sales attribution:** Use direct, indirect, and attributed GMV according to their respective metric definitions. Do not assume that new fields are equivalent to removed fields on a one-to-one basis or add GMV metrics with different attribution definitions together.

### Prepare Your Integration
Select the APIs and new metrics that meet your business needs, then use the mapping tables below to update request parameters, response parsing, and mappings to your data models and reports. Applications using Get Shop Video Performance Details should prioritize migration to the batch API and query product-level details through Get Shop Video Product Performance List. Validate single-video and multiple-video requests, errors for individual videos, date granularity, pagination, current-day and historical data, field types, and missing-value handling. The previous API will be retired within three months of this release, so migrate as soon as possible.
### Old-to-New Field Mapping
The following tables list request and response field mappings by API. Response paths start at `data`; braces denote fields at the same level, and fields listed together may share a prefix. A dash means there is no corresponding field. Changes in metric definitions and cross-API migrations indicate migration options, not numerical equivalence. Previous video details fields use version 202509 as the baseline.
#### Get Shop Performance
| Previous Field / Parameter | New Field / Migration Target | Change Type | Migration Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| data.performance.intervals[].sales.gmv.overall.{amount,currency} | data.performance.intervals[].sales.gmv.{amount,currency} | Flattened structure | The overall layer is removed. Read amounts and currencies directly from gmv.amount and gmv.currency. |
| data.performance.intervals[].sales.gmv.breakdowns[]（gmv、type） | data.performance.intervals[].breakdowns; sales.live_gmv, sales.video_gmv, sales.product_card_attributed_gmv, etc. (all within the same interval) | Previous structure removed; new breakdown dimensions added | The previous array provided LIVE／VIDEO／PRODUCT_CARD breakdowns. The new breakdowns supports content types, seller／creator channels, and source attribution. Select fields based on your reporting definitions; the new structure as a whole is not an equivalent replacement for the previous array. |
| data.performance.intervals[].sales.gross_revenue (including overall and breakdowns) | — | Removed; no confirmed equivalent field | The previous metric included customer payments and platform subsidies, less applicable taxes. It does not directly correspond to gmv, gmv_with_tax, or gmv_with_tiktok_co_funding. |
| data.performance.intervals[].sales.avg_customers_count | — (related new field: data.performance.intervals[].sales.customers_count) | Removed; no equivalent field | The previous field represented the daily average number of unique paying customers. customers_count represents unique paying customers during the query period. The two fields are not interchangeable. |
