| Previous Field / Parameter | New Field / Migration Target | Change Type | Migration Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| — (new) | data.performance.intervals[] and data.performance.overall: avg_order_value, avg_sku_order_value, customers_count, orders_count, sku_orders_count, live_attributed_gmv, video_attributed_gmv, product_card_attributed_gmv, product_clicks, product_impressions, unique_clicks, unique_product_impressions | Added to both result types | The existing response structure is retained. Extend parsing for the new metrics in hourly intervals and daily totals separately. |

#### Get Shop Video Performance Overview
| Previous Field / Parameter | New Field / Migration Target | Change Type | Migration Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| data.performance.intervals[].gmv | data.performance.intervals[].video_gmv / video_attributed_gmv / video_indirect_gmv | Previous field removed; new sales metrics with different definitions added | Select video GMV, video-attributed GMV, or video indirect GMV based on your business needs. This is not a field rename. All monetary objects use amount and currency. |
| data.performance.intervals[].click_through_rate | data.performance.intervals[].ctr | Previous field removed; corresponding ratio field added | Both field descriptions define the metric as product clicks / video views. The new ctr explicitly returns a raw decimal; for example, 0.0528 represents 5.28%. Update the field name and display format. Do not display the raw decimal directly as a percentage. |
| data.performance.intervals[].sku_orders | data.performance.intervals[].video_attributed_sku_orders | Previous field removed; attributed order metric added | The previous field counted paid SKU orders placed directly from shoppable videos. The new field counts paid SKU orders attributed to videos. Their sales attribution definitions differ, so they are not numerically equivalent replacements. |
| data.performance.intervals[].avg_customers | data.performance.intervals[].avg_daily_customers | Previous field removed; corresponding daily average customer field added | Both fields are described as the daily average number of customers, and both use Int64. Update the parsing path to avg_daily_customers. |

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

The field name and monetary object structure remain unchanged. Review your reporting definitions against the updated description. |
| data.videos[].click_through_rate | data.videos[].click_through_rate (field name unchanged) | Return format and metric description clarified | Product clicks / video views, returned as a raw decimal. The description and example are corrected to use decimal notation. This field is retained in the video list; do not migrate it to ctr as you would for the video overview. |

The previous API will be retired within three months of the 2026-09-28 release date. Complete migration before it is retired. |
| Path: video_id (String, required) | Query: video_ids (List<Int64>, required) | Parameter location, name, and type changed | Replace the single video_id in the path with the video_ids query parameter list. Use a list even when querying a single video. |
| Query: start_date_ge / end_date_lt | Query: start_date_ge / end_date_lt | Retained | Both are required String parameters. Dates use the shop's registered timezone, with an inclusive start date and an exclusive end date. |

Monetary objects continue to use amount and currency. ctr is product clicks / video product impressions. |
| data.performance.intervals[].traffic.{views,new_followers,shares,comments,likes} | data.videos[].performance.intervals[].traffic.{views,new_followers,shares,comments,likes} | Path changed | Read traffic and engagement metrics per video. Field names are retained. |

Query product details separately using GET /analytics/202509/shop_videos/{video_id}/products/performance and handle pagination. |

In that list, gpm is GMV per 1,000 product impressions, and ctr is product clicks / product impressions. This differs from the previous direct-order conversion definition. |

This is not an equivalent field rename. |

Do not omit the parameter or pass ALL, and do not use LINKED_ACCOUNTS from the LIVE overview. |

Both new ctr and live_ctr return raw decimal strings. Review their definitions separately rather than carrying over previous values directly. |

| data.live_stream_sessions[].sales_performance.gmv (including amount and currency) | data.live_stream_sessions[].sales_performance.live_gmv／live_indirect_gmv／live_attributed_gmv | Previous field removed; separate GMV definitions introduced | The new fields provide direct, indirect, and attributed GMV, respectively. Select the definition you need. No field is stated to be numerically equivalent to the previous gmv. |
| data.live_stream_sessions[].sales_performance.sku_orders | data.live_stream_sessions[].sales_performance.live_attributed_sku_orders | Previous field removed; attribution definition changed | The new field counts paid SKU orders attributed to the individual LIVE session. |
| data.live_stream_sessions[].sales_performance.items_sold | data.live_stream_sessions[].sales_performance.live_attributed_items_sold | Previous field removed; attribution definition changed | The new field counts items sold attributed to the individual LIVE session. |
| data.live_stream_sessions[].sales_performance.customers | data.live_stream_sessions[].sales_performance.attributed_customers | Previous field removed; customer attribution definition changed | The previous field counted customers who placed orders directly in the LIVE session. The new field counts customers attributed to that LIVE session. This is not simply a rename. |
| data.live_stream_sessions[].sales_performance.click_to_order_rate | data.live_stream_sessions[].traffic.ctor／traffic.ctor_sku_order | Previous field removed; path, format, and metric selection changed | ctor is the click-to-order conversion rate, returned as a raw decimal string. ctor_sku_order is explicitly defined as attributed SKU orders／product clicks. The previous field used a percentage. Update the display format and review the business definition. |
| data.live_stream_sessions[].sales_performance.24h_live_gmv (including amount and currency) | — | Removed; no confirmed equivalent field | The new live_attributed_gmv is not an equivalent replacement for the previous 24-hour-window metric. Stop using the previous field mapping. |
| data.live_stream_sessions[].username | data.live_stream_sessions[].creator.user_name | Field name and path changed | Read the creator username from the creator structure, which also provides author_type, creator_country, nick_name, and open_id. |

## 2026-09-25 · Products · For MY Market: Key Attributes Become Mandatory for Selected Hi-Po Categories

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/m8p624yr

#### Summary
Malaysia will roll out mandatory key attributes for selected hi-po categories in **30 September, 2026**. For Health, Sports & Outdoor, and Tools & Hardware, selected attributes will change from **optional** to **required**. For Household Appliances, TikTok Shop will add a new structured attribute, **Energy Efficiency Star Rating**, and require it for relevant products. This is a category configuration update that affects seller-center and partner-powered product creation flows.
#### What is changing?
| Area | Change |
| --- | --- |
| Health | Selected key attributes will become required during product creation. |
| Sports & Outdoor | Selected key attributes will become required during product creation. |
| Tools &Hardware | Selected key attributes will become required during product creation. |
| Household Appliances | A new structured attribute, Energy Efficiency Star Rating, will be added and required for relevant products. |
This update is intended to improve structured product data completeness and support better search, filtering, product navigation, and recommendation quality.
#### Which markets are affected?
**Malaysia (MY)** only.
#### Which categories are affected?

* Health
* Sports & Outdoor
* Tools & Hardware
* Household Appliances

#### Detailed attribute scope
The following details can help partners, ISVs, ERPs, and API integrators update attribute mapping, validation, and listing logic for the MY rollout.
##### Health — Optional to required
This change covers **15 L3 categories** and **4 attributes**.
| Attribute | L2 scope | L3 coverage | Applicable L3 categories |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Health Benefits | Nutrition & Wellness | 3 L3s | Beauty Supplement / Fitness Supplements / Vitamins, Minerals & Wellness Supplements |
| Product Form | Nutrition & Wellness; OTC Medications & Treatments   | 13 L3s total | **Nutrition & Wellness (4)**: Beauty Supplement / Fitness Supplements / Vitamins, Minerals & Wellness Supplements / Weight Management Supplements  **OTC Medications & Treatments (9)**: Allergies, Sinus & Asthma / Antifungals / Baby & Child Medicine / Coughs & Colds / Cuts & Wounds / Digestion & Nausea / Eczema, Psoriasis & Rosacea Care / Pain Relief / Scars & Stretchmarks |
| Item Type | Medical Supplies | 2 L3s | First Aid Supplies / Pill Dispensers |
| Medicine Type | OTC Medications & Treatments | 1 L3 | Baby & Child Medicine |
##### Sports & Outdoor — Optional to required
This change covers **4 L3 categories** and **4 attributes**.
| Attribute | L2 scope | L3 coverage | Applicable L3 category |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Shuttlecocks Type | Ball Sports | 1 L3 | Badminton |
| Fitness Machine Type | Fitness | 1 L3 | Exercise Machines |
| Clothing Type | Sport & Outdoor Clothing | 1 L3 | Sport Thermal Underwear |
| Bra Style | Sport & Outdoor Clothing | 1 L3 | Sports Bras |
##### Tools & Hardware — Optional to required
This change covers **40 L3 categories** and **2 attributes**.
| Attribute | L2 scope | L3 coverage | Applicable L3 categories |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Warranty Duration | Power Tools; Garden Tools | 20 L3s total | **Power Tools (16)**: Air Compressors / Angle Grinders / Blowers / Electric Drills / Electric Saws / Electric Screwdrivers / Electric Wrenches / Glue Guns / Heat Guns / Nail Guns / Polishers / Power Tool Sets / Pressure Washers / Specialty Power Tools / Spray Guns / Wood Routers  **Garden Tools (4)**: Chainsaws / Grass Trimmers / Lawn Mowers / Leaf Blowers & Vacuums |
| Warranty Type | Hand Tools; Hardware; Garden Tools; Power Tools; Soldering Equipment; Tool Organizers; Measuring Tools | 20 L3s total | **Hand Tools (6)**: Axes / Chisels / Hammers / Saws / Tool Kits / Wrenches  **Hardware (3)**: Clamps / Furniture Hardware / Window Hardware  **Garden Tools (3)**: Garden Cleaning Tools / Gardening Gloves & Protective Gear / Pruning Tools  **Power Tools (1)**: Power Tool Accessories  **Soldering Equipment (3)**: Soldering Stations / Welders / Welding Accessories  **Tool Organizers (3)**: Tool Bags / Tool Cases & Boxes / Tool Racks & Bars  **Measuring Tools (1)**: Hand Measuring Tools |
##### Household Appliances — New attribute plus required enforcement
This change covers **8 L3 categories** and **1 new attribute**.
| Attribute | L2 scope | L3 coverage | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Energy Efficiency Star Rating | Large Home Appliances | 8 L3s | **New attribute**: this field does not exist on the platform today and requires a new attribute creation flow before required enforcement. The scope includes categories such as Air Conditioner, Refrigerators, TVs, and Washing Machines & Dryers. |
#### Who is affected?

* Partners, ISVs, and ERPs that create or update MY product listings.
* API integrators that submit product data for sellers in the affected MY categories.
* MY sellers using partner tools or direct API-based listing flows.

#### Which version is applicable?
No separate OpenAPI version change is specified in the PRD. This is a **category configuration rollout** for MY and will apply when the change goes live in **30 September, 2026.**
#### What action is required?

* Review your MY product creation and product update flows for the affected categories.
* Make sure your integrations can populate all newly required attributes before submitting products.
* Add support for Energy Efficiency Star Rating in Household Appliances.
* Validate request handling, field mapping, and submission logic before the final rollout date.
* Watch for the final merchant announcement and cutover timeline before switching production traffic.

## 2026-09-24 · Analytics · US SPS Update: Impact on Existing APIs

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/46di8q34

**Effective date: October 15, 2026**
TikTok Shop will launch the new Shop Performance Score (SPS) in the US on October 15, 2026. The new SPS page will replace the existing page in Seller Center. This update revises the metrics and calculations for Product Satisfaction and Logistics & Fulfillment; Customer Service remains unchanged. For details, see the [New SPS Trial-run Guide](https://seller-us.tiktok.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=7105568016090887).
The impact on API integrations is as follows:

* **Existing SPS APIs will remain available and will not be retired as part of this update.** From October 15, scores and associated scoring rules from these APIs will be for reference only. They should no longer be used to assess shop performance or guide improvements under the new SPS framework.
* **Review how your application displays and uses existing SPS data.** If your application continues to display data from existing APIs, clearly label it “Previous SPS version — for reference only.” Review any alerts or recommendations based on the previous scoring rules. Refer sellers to the new SPS page in Seller Center for current scores, scoring rules, and improvement guidance.
* **New SPS APIs will be released as soon as possible.** The release date and integration instructions will be announced separately.

## 2026-09-24 · Products · [US] Product Listing API: Category Attribute Updates for Auto Parts, Tires, Motorcycles, and Engine Oils

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/ksfhhcre

**Planned effective date: September 30, 2026**
**Market: United States**
TikTok Shop plans to update product attributes for selected US categories covering auto parts, tires, motorcycles, and engine oils. These updates introduce additional vehicle fitment, product specification, and compliance information.
Developers listing products in the affected categories should review their attribute configurations and prepare to submit product information according to the updated requirements.
**What is changing**
| Affected categories | Attribute updates |
| --- | --- |
| **Auto parts:** Selected leaf categories listed in the change request, including suspension, steering, cooling, ignition, fuel, exhaust, transmission, and braking components | New attributes include fitment and installation recommendations; vehicle year, make, model, and engine; drivetrain configuration; manufacturer part number (MPN); OEM part number; warranty; manufacturer; Non-DOT or off-road-only parts; EPA numbers; and CARB compliance status. Category-specific applicability and mandatory requirements are pending confirmation. |
| **Tires:** Trailer, Racing, Passenger Car, Motorbike, Motor Home & RV, Lorry & SUV, Kart Racing, Golf Cart, Commercial Truck, and Agricultural Tractor & Farm Equipment | Attributes will be added or assigned to these categories for installation recommendations, highway use, tire class and specifications, identification number, year of manufacture, warranty, and registration form link. The detailed configuration also includes EPA numbers and CARB compliance status. Mandatory requirements are listed below. |
| **Motorcycles:** Manual Transmission Motorcycles (`847632`), Automatic Transmission Motorcycles (`847760`), and Electric Motorcycles (`847888`) | New attributes include Off Road Warning, OHV Warning, DOT Warning, FCC Numbers, Vehicle Make, Vehicle Model, Year, Engine displacement (cc), Fuel type, Transmission type, EPA numbers, and CARB compliance status. Category-specific applicability and mandatory requirements are pending confirmation. |
| **Engine oils:** Car Engine Oils (`2276880`) | Attribute additions and removals are planned. The specific attributes and any replacement mappings are pending confirmation. |

**Tire attribute requirements**
The following attributes are marked as mandatory for all 10 tire categories in the detailed configuration:
| Attribute | Input requirements |
| --- | --- |
| Installation Recommendation | Select the predefined professional installation recommendation |
| For Highway Use | Select `Yes` or `No` |
| Tire Class | Select `P` or `LT`, or enter a custom value |
| Tire width (mm), Aspect Ratio, Tire Construction, Wheel diameter/size (inch), Load index, Speed Rating, Season | Custom input |
| Tire Identification Number, Year of Manufacture, Warranty, Tire registration form link | Custom input |
| EPA establishment number (`102692`), EPA registration number (`102694`) | Custom input |
| California Air Resources Board (CARB) Compliance (`102697`) | Select `Compliant`, `Exempt`, or `Non-compliant` |

Additionally, **UTQG Treadwear rating, UTQG Traction rating, and UTQG Temperature rating** will be added to Passenger Car (`1462544`), Motorbike (`1462672`), and Lorry & SUV (`1462928`).
**Recommended developer actions**

1. Once the updates take effect, call [Get Attributes](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-attributes-202309) for the relevant leaf category and refresh your local attribute configurations, caches, and value mappings.
2. Submit attribute values through `product_attributes` according to the mandatory and conditional requirements returned by the API. If your application uses fixed listing templates, review the collection and validation of newly introduced attributes.
3. Validate product listing flows for the affected categories, focusing on missing mandatory attributes, predefined values, and custom input.

**Internal note — resolve before publication:** Confirm the exact effective time and time zone, affected APIs and versions, compatibility impact, handling of existing products and edit requests, and the specific engine oil attribute additions and removals. Detailed auto parts, motorcycle, and engine oil requirements could not be verified due to access restrictions. The current change requests remain under review and do not support a confirmed “no integration changes required” statement.

## 2026-09-24 · Other · Shipping Template Widget Sunset

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/9kaaw0r4

#### 1. Background
The Shipping template widget lives under **Development Kits → Widgets** in Partner Center. It is an embeddable component that lets sellers who choose "shipping by sellers" create and maintain shipping templates directly inside an ISV app, without going to Seller Center.
(ảnh: https://p16-arcosite-sg.ibyteimg.com/tos-alisg-i-k9wyc2ijk0-sg/12b1814050cb4b20988856237652c443~tplv-k9wyc2ijk0-image.image)

The widget is a **2024 legacy version**. Compared with the current shipping template capability in Seller Center:

* The underlying data model is different. Fields and template structure in the legacy model no longer map cleanly to the current model.
* The interaction flow is different. The current flow ties into warehouses, logistics service providers, region restrictions, and other capabilities that the legacy widget cannot carry.
* Because of that, continuing to use the widget creates **availability risk**: templates saved through the widget can be incomplete or incompatible with the current flow, which surfaces as **logistics services being unreachable** and, ultimately, **buyers failing to place orders**.

The purpose of this sunset is to consolidate shipping template configuration into **Seller Center**, so the widget can no longer fall behind the latest logistics flow and disrupt orders.
#### 2. Scope
| Dimension | Scope |
| --- | --- |
| Component | Partner Center Widgets → **Shipping template** (2024 shipping template widget) |
| Markets | **All markets** |
| Sellers | **All sellers**, both local and cross-border |
| Integrators | Every ISV / developer whose app embeds the Shipping template widget |
| Not affected | Other widgets (Warehouse settings, Shipping by TikTok, Tax information, Product optimizer, Data compass, etc.); the shipping template capability in Seller Center itself is unchanged |
| New permission scope | No. Already authorized apps do not need to re-authorize.   |

   **Why this is a breaking change**: after October 31, 2026, the Shipping template widget will no longer be available. Any ISV app that still embeds the widget will see **load and save failures**, and templates configured through it may not take effect in the current logistics flow, which puts **logistics availability and order completion** at risk.

#### 3. Replacement Path After Sunset
After sunset, shipping template configuration lives **entirely in Seller Center**. Partner Center no longer offers a widget for this. ISV apps should move from "configure in-app through the widget" to "redirect the seller to Seller Center".
| Scenario | Replacement |
| --- | --- |
| Seller creates a new shipping template inside the ISV app | Replace the widget slot with a redirect entry: explain that this capability has moved to Seller Center → Shipping templates, and provide a one-click link to open it. |
| Seller views or edits an existing template inside the ISV app | Redirect to the same Seller Center page. Templates that already exist in Seller Center continue to feed the current checkout and logistics flow. |
| In-app logistics-plan editing flow | Keep the rest of the logistics-plan configuration in the app, but replace the "edit shipping template" step with a Seller Center redirect. Prompt the seller to save their in-app progress before the redirect. |
#### 4. What ISVs Need to Do

1. **Inventory all entry points**: identify every place in your app that embeds the Shipping template widget — standalone template pages, onboarding flows, logistics-plan setup flows, etc.
2. **Replace with a redirect**: by October 31, 2026, turn every widget slot into a **redirect entry to the Seller Center shipping template page**, and explain the change in-context. Do not leave sellers with a blank or broken widget.
3. **Update copy and guidance**: update in-app onboarding, help center, and any documentation that tells sellers to "configure shipping templates inside this app" so it reads "go to Seller Center to configure shipping templates".
4. **Notify existing sellers**: for sellers who previously created templates through the widget, proactively ask them to open Seller Center and check whether the existing templates match the current flow, to avoid legacy issues like wrong delivery scope or wrong shipping fees after sunset.
5. **Ship the update**: submit the new app version through the standard release flow and complete rollout **before October 31, 2026**.

**Checklist**

- [ ] All in-app entry points that embed the Shipping template widget have been listed.
- [ ] Each entry point has been switched to a Seller Center redirect.
- [ ] Onboarding, help docs, and in-app copy have been updated.
- [ ] Existing sellers have been notified to review or migrate their templates.
- [ ] The new app version is fully rolled out before October 31, 2026.

#### 5. Timeline
| Date | Milestone |
| --- | --- |
| Now — October 31, 2026 | **Migration window**: ISVs remove the widget, switch to Seller Center redirects, and notify sellers. The widget still loads, but you should switch as soon as possible. |
| **October 31, 2026** | **Shipping template widget is officially sunset**. It is removed from the Partner Center Widgets list, and any slot in an ISV app that still embeds it will fail to load. |
| After sunset | All shipping template configuration lives in Seller Center. ISVs guide sellers there through redirect entries. |
#### 6. FAQ
**Q1. Is only the widget being retired, or is the shipping template feature going away too?**
Only the Partner Center Shipping template widget is being retired. **The shipping template feature itself is not going away**. Sellers can still configure and manage shipping templates in **Seller Center**, and those templates continue to feed the current checkout and logistics flow.
**Q2. Is there a replacement widget?**
**No replacement widget will be offered** as part of this sunset. For any scenario that used to rely on the widget, redirect sellers to Seller Center.
**Q3. What happens to templates that sellers already created through this widget?**
Existing templates are not deleted by the widget being retired, but because the legacy model differs from the current model, we strongly recommend asking sellers to **review or update** those templates in Seller Center to avoid unreachable logistics services or wrong shipping fee calculations.
**Q4. Does the sunset invalidate our existing app authorization?**
No. The sunset only removes the widget. It does not change any OAuth scope, and authorized apps do not need to re-authorize.
**Q5. Will there be a new shipping template API?**
This notice is only about the widget sunset and does not introduce any new shipping template API. Shipping template configuration goes through Seller Center. If any API-side changes come later, they will be announced through a separate Partner Center changelog entry.
**Q6. Is Shipping by TikTok affected?**
No. Shipping by TikTok is a separate widget and is not affected by this sunset.


---

## 2026-09-22 · Finance · Finance Transaction APIs: New Fee & Tax Properties for Growth Package and Auto-Post Shoppable Video

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/8he1k3et

#### Summary
Three Finance transaction APIs — **Get Transactions by Order**, **Get Transactions by Statement**, and **Get Unsettled Transactions** — add **8 new response properties** under the existing `fee_tax_breakdown.fee` and `fee_tax_breakdown.tax` objects. Four properties report the fee and sales tax charged for the three new **Smart Promotion Growth Packages** (New Customer / Store GMV / Target Product GMV), and four report the commission fee and sales tax for orders attributed to **AI-generated, auto-post shoppable videos**.
All properties are purely additive — no existing field is removed, renamed or re-typed, and there is no request parameter change on any of the three APIs.

#### Impact
| Item | Detail |
| --- | --- |
| **Impacted endpoints** | Response only (new fields), no request change:    * Get Transactions by Order   * Get Transactions by Statement   * Get Unsettled Transactions  |
| **Impacted version(s)** | `202501` (Get Transactions by Order, Get Transactions by Statement), `202507` (Get Unsettled Transactions) |
| **Impacted market(s)** | Growth Package **fee** fields (`*_growth_package_fee`): **US / JP / EU / UK**.  Growth Package **tax** fields (`*_tax` / `*_sales_tax`): **US only** for now  |
| **Seller type** |  L2L and cross-border (CB) sellers enrolled in the corresponding Smart Promotion Growth Package.  |
| **Permission scope** | No new permission scope. Apps already authorized for the existing Finance/transaction scopes continue to work without re-authorization. |
| **Effective date** | Already live in the response schema, aligned with the Growth Package feature rollout (target: end of August 2026 per the product roadmap). Values are `"0"` for shops/orders not enrolled in or not attributed to the corresponding program. |
| **Compatibility** | **Non-breaking**. Only 8 new response properties are added under `fee_tax_breakdown.fee` / `fee_tax_breakdown.tax`; no existing field, type or required-ness changes. |

#### Field-level diff
| Field | Category | Location | Old | New |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `new_customer_growth_package_fee` | Fee | `fee_tax_breakdown.fee` | Not returned | `string` amount, additive |
| `store_gmv_growth_package_fee` | Fee | `fee_tax_breakdown.fee` | Not returned | `string` amount, additive |
| `target_product_gmv_growth_package_fee` | Fee | `fee_tax_breakdown.fee` | Not returned | `string` amount, additive |
| `auto_post_shoppable_video_commission_fee` | Fee | `fee_tax_breakdown.fee` | Not returned | `string` amount, additive |
| `new_customer_growth_package_fee_tax` | Tax | `fee_tax_breakdown.tax` | Not returned | `string` amount, additive |
| `store_gmv_growth_package_fee_sales_tax` | Tax | `fee_tax_breakdown.tax` | Not returned | `string` amount, additive |
| `target_product_gmv_growth_package_tax` | Tax | `fee_tax_breakdown.tax` | Not returned | `string` amount, additive |
| `auto_post_shoppable_video_sales_tax` | Tax | `fee_tax_breakdown.tax` | Not returned | `string` amount, additive |

#### Field definition
##### Growth Package fee & tax
These three fees correspond to TikTok Shop's Smart Promotion **Growth Package** add-ons: sellers can additionally enroll in one or more growth packages on top of the base Smart Promotion package, and TikTok Shop's algorithm allocates subsidies toward the seller's chosen objective. The fee is charged based on `platform discount (co-funded label) × seller-borne percentage`, and covers **product-price subsidies only** — shipping subsidies are excluded.

* `new_customer_growth_package_fee` — fee for discounts funded through the New Customer Growth Package, used to attract new customers to the shop.
* `store_gmv_growth_package_fee` — fee for discounts funded through the Store GMV Growth Package, used to grow store-wide sales.
* `target_product_gmv_growth_package_fee` — fee for discounts funded through the Target Product GMV Growth Package, scoped to seller-selected products.
* `new_customer_growth_package_fee_tax` / `store_gmv_growth_package_fee_sales_tax` / `target_product_gmv_growth_package_tax` — the sales tax charged on the corresponding growth package fee above. Currently populated for **US** sellers; for EU/UK/JP the tax is already netted into the fee amount, so these three tax fields return `"0"` there.

##### Auto-post shoppable video

* `auto_post_shoppable_video_commission_fee` — the commission fee charged to the seller for orders attributed to AI-generated, auto-post shoppable videos.
* `auto_post_shoppable_video_sales_tax` — the sales tax applied to the auto-post shoppable video commission fee above.

#### Samples
Response sample — Get Transactions by Statement (`fee_tax_breakdown`, relevant fields only):
```JSON
{
  "code": 0,
  "data": {
    "transactions": [
      {
        "fee_tax_amount": "-8",
        "fee_tax_breakdown": {
          "fee": {
            "new_customer_growth_package_fee": "-3",
            "store_gmv_growth_package_fee": "-2",
            "target_product_gmv_growth_package_fee": "0",
            "auto_post_shoppable_video_commission_fee": "-1"
          },
          "tax": {
            "new_customer_growth_package_fee_tax": "-0.3",
            "store_gmv_growth_package_fee_sales_tax": "-0.2",
            "target_product_gmv_growth_package_tax": "0",
            "auto_post_shoppable_video_sales_tax": "-0.1"
          }
        }
      }
    ]
  },
  "message": "Success"
}
```

Get Transactions by Order and Get Unsettled Transactions return the same `fee_tax_breakdown.fee` / `.tax` shape, nested under `data.sku_transactions[]` and `data.transactions[]` respectively.

#### Is this a breaking change?
**No — this is a non-breaking, additive change.**

* All 8 fields are new **response** properties only; there is no request-side change to any of the three APIs.
* No existing field is removed, renamed or re-typed, and no default value or existing behavior changes.
* No new permission scope is introduced, so no re-authorization is required.

#### Developer action items

* **If you don't offer or track Growth Packages / auto-post shoppable video**: no action is required. The 8 new fields simply return `"0"` and your existing integration keeps working unchanged.
* **If you want to reconcile Growth Package spend**: read `fee_tax_breakdown.fee.new_customer_growth_package_fee` / `store_gmv_growth_package_fee` / `target_product_gmv_growth_package_fee` and the matching `*_tax` / `*_sales_tax` fields into your cost/P&L reporting.
* **If you track auto-post shoppable video commission**: read `fee_tax_breakdown.fee.auto_post_shoppable_video_commission_fee` and `fee_tax_breakdown.tax.auto_post_shoppable_video_sales_tax`.
* **If your app has a strict response schema**: add these 8 fields to your `fee_tax_breakdown.fee` / `.tax` models as optional string amounts, and tolerate additional unknown fields TikTok Shop may add in the future.
* No API version upgrade or re-declaration is required — keep calling the existing `202501` / `202507` endpoints as before.

#### References

* [Get Transactions by Order](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-transactions-by-order)
* [Get Transactions by Statement](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-transactions-by-statement)
* [Get Unsettled Transactions](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-unsettled-transactions)
* [Finance API overview](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/finance-api-overview)

## 2026-09-18 · Products · Product APIs: Mandatory Wig Material and Conditional Certification Requirements (US)

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/ewba9wve

### What is changing?
Starting **November 21, 2026**, the **Wig material** attribute (ID: `102428`) will become mandatory when creating or editing products in the affected US wig categories through APIs. The attribute is currently optional. This update is intended to improve the accuracy and completeness of wig material information.
Developers must collect the material selection and submit any additional information required by that selection:
| Wig material | Requirement |
| --- | --- |
| **Human hair** | Provide the human hair percentage through the applicable conditional product attribute. This percentage requirement is already in place. |
| **100% human hair** | Submit a human hair content test report through the applicable product certification. |
| **Synthetic hair** | No additional human hair percentage or test report is required by this material rule. Other applicable category requirements still apply. |

Once the API exemption period ends, missing the mandatory material attribute or its applicable supporting information may cause product creation or editing to fail validation. Update both your seller-facing forms and API request construction before enforcement begins.
### Which markets are affected?
This update applies to the **United States (US)** market and the wig categories listed in the appendix. The category configuration covers local, cross-border POP, and fully managed sellers. This announcement addresses API-based listing integrations.
### Who is affected?
Developers, ISVs, and seller developers whose integrations create or edit products in the affected US wig categories, including integrations that cache category attributes or certification requirements.
### Which version is applicable?
This update changes category validation requirements and uses existing product attribute and certification fields. Review the following APIs in your integration:
| API | Version | Required adaptation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Get Attributes](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-attributes-202309) | 202309 | Refresh the affected categories' attributes and allowed values. Evaluate `requirement_conditions` for conditional attributes such as human hair percentage. |
| [Get Category Rules](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-category-rules-202309) | 202309 | Retrieve `product_certifications`, including certification IDs, document requirements, and `requirement_conditions`. |
| [Create Product](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/create-product-202309) | 202309 | Include Wig material and applicable conditional attributes in `product_attributes`; include the required test report in `certifications`. |
| [Edit Product](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/edit-product-202309) | 202309 | Ensure affected product edits satisfy the material and conditional certification requirements. |
| [Edit Product](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/edit-product-202509) | 202509 | Ensure affected product edits satisfy the material and conditional certification requirements. |
| [Check Product Listing](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/check-product-listing-202309) | 202309 | Validate the material attribute, conditional attributes, and certifications in the intended listing payload. |

**API enforcement date: November 21, 2026.** The rollout begins on September 21, 2026, with a two-month exemption for the API channel. Complete your API integration updates **before November 21, 2026**.
### What action is required?

* **Refresh category metadata.** Retrieve the current attribute and certification rules for each affected leaf category in the relevant shop context. Update cached schemas and evaluate conditional requirements when sellers change the material selection.
* **Make Wig material mandatory in your listing forms.** Use attribute ID `102428` and the built-in value IDs returned by Get Attributes. This attribute is a single-select field; do not substitute free-text material descriptions for the supported values.
* **Submit the corresponding request data.** Put the material selection and any required human hair percentage in `product_attributes`. For **100% human hair**, retrieve the applicable certification ID from Get Category Rules, upload the report using [Upload Product File](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/upload-product-file-202309) or [Upload Product Image](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/upload-product-image-202309) as appropriate, and include the returned file ID or image URI in `certifications`. Follow the returned document requirements.
* **Review existing products.** Identify products in the affected categories with missing material information or supporting documents. Collect the missing information so subsequent edits can meet the requirements. Do not automatically default products to a material value without seller confirmation.
* **Test before the enforcement date.** Cover all three material selections, missing material, missing human hair percentage, missing test reports, and changes between material values. Use Check Product Listing where applicable and confirm both creation and editing flows meet the updated rules.

**Test report requirements for 100% human hair**
The report must be issued by a professional testing institution with hair-product testing capabilities and include:

* A clear identification of the tested product, such as a wig, hair extension, or hair accessory.
* The full name of the commissioning entity.
* A human hair content test.
* The report number, sample quantity, sample receipt date, test date, sample description, test conclusion, and the testing institution's inspection stamp.

### Appendix: Affected categories
| Category ID | Category name |
| --- | --- |
| 1080200 | Synthetic Braided Full Lace Wigs |
| 1080072 | Synthetic Braided Half Lace Wigs |
| 1079944 | Synthetic Braided T Part Lace Wigs |
| 812048 | Human Hair Lace Closure Wigs |
| 812176 | Human Hair Custom Lace Wigs |
| 812304 | Human Hair Full Machine Wigs |
| 812560 | Synthetic Braided Lace Wigs |
| 812688 | Synthetic Full Machine Wigs |
| 812816 | Synthetic Lace Front Wigs |
| 908680 | Human Hair Silk Base Lace Wigs |
| 908808 | Synthetic Costume Wigs |
| 811536 | Human Hair T Part Lace Wigs |
| 811664 | Human Hair Full Lace Wigs |
| 811792 | Human Hair 360 Lace Wigs |
| 811920 | Human Hair Lace Front Wigs |

## 2026-09-16 · Products · Inventory Changed by Shop: New Webhook for SKU Inventory Updates

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/nn6csp5o

#### What is changing?
TikTok Shop is introducing the **Inventory Changed by Shop** webhook (`type: 71`) to notify developers when SKU inventory quantities change.
This webhook covers inventory changes caused by order creation, cancellation, and shipment; manual adjustments in Seller Center; inventory updates through OpenAPIs; inventory locking and release for marketing campaigns or creator collaborations; and automatic replenishment by the system.
Each notification includes:

* **Affected product and SKU:** Identifies the shop, product, and SKU associated with the inventory change.
* **Post-change inventory snapshot:** Provides total inventory, available inventory, committed inventory, in-shop inventory, and inventory locked for campaigns or creator collaborations.
* **Individual change details:** Provides the trigger source, occurrence time, and quantity changes for each inventory category.
* **Idempotency identifiers:** Includes `event_id` for event-level deduplication and `idempotency_key` for deduplicating individual change operations.

Developers can use these notifications to synchronize inventory and track the reasons for inventory changes, reducing the need to infer stock movements from separate business events.
#### Which markets are affected?
This webhook applies to all markets, as specified in the [Inventory changed announcement](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/7u0ofsrd).
#### Who is affected?
This update applies to developers and service providers using TikTok Shop APIs to manage or synchronize merchants’ product inventory, including integrations with ERP, inventory management, and multichannel commerce systems.
#### Which version is applicable?
This is a new webhook event and does not introduce a new API version. Notifications are delivered to the webhook URL configured for your application. See the [Inventory changed announcement](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/7u0ofsrd).
#### What action is required?
We recommend integrating this webhook if your application needs to track SKU inventory changes.
Recommended actions:

* Configure your application to receive and process the **Inventory Changed by Shop** webhook (`type: 71`).
* Update your webhook handler to parse `quantity_snapshot_after_change` and the `change_detail` array, including the trigger source, occurrence time, and quantity deltas.
* Use `event_id` and `idempotency_key` to prevent duplicate processing at the event and individual-change levels.
* Distinguish inventory snapshots from quantity deltas. A snapshot represents inventory after the change; a delta represents an increase or decrease. Avoid applying both to the same inventory balance.
* Test inventory synchronization across order events, manual adjustments, API updates, campaign and creator inventory reservations, and automatic replenishment.

For the complete payload structure, field descriptions, and event example, refer to the [Inventory Changed by Shop Webhook documentation](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/qk9yta93).


---

## 2026-09-16 · Products · Product APIs: Selling Format Support Added for Fixed-Price Sales and LIVE Auctions

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/tfdpn5tm

#### Summary
Product APIs now support the `selling_format` field, allowing developers to specify whether a product supports fixed-price sales only or both fixed-price sales and LIVE auctions.
The field is added to the request bodies of Create Product, Edit Product (202309 and 202509), and Check Product Listing. Get Product and Search Products also return this field, with an additional response value, `LIVE_AUCTION`, to identify products that support LIVE auctions only.
#### What is changing
**New request body field**
The following APIs add the optional `selling_format` field to their request bodies:
| API | Change |
| --- | --- |
| Create Product | Specify the selling format when creating a product. |
| Edit Product (202309) | Specify the selling format when editing a product. |
| Edit Product (202509) | Specify the selling format when editing a product. |
| Check Product Listing | Include the selling format when checking a product listing. |

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `selling_format` | String | No | Specifies the product’s selling format. Defaults to `FIXED_PRICE`. |

Supported **request values**:
| Value | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `FIXED_PRICE` | The product supports fixed-price sales only. This is the default selling format. |
| `FIXED_PRICE_AND_LIVE_AUCTION` | The product supports both fixed-price sales and LIVE auctions. A starting bid price is required for LIVE auctions. |

**New response field**
Get Product and Search Products add `selling_format` to their product response information.
| API | Field | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Get Product | `selling_format` | String | Returns the product’s selling format. |
| Search Products | `selling_format` | String | Returns the selling format for each product. |

Supported **response values**:
| Value | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `FIXED_PRICE` | The product supports fixed-price sales only. |
| `FIXED_PRICE_AND_LIVE_AUCTION` | The product supports both fixed-price sales and LIVE auctions. |
| `LIVE_AUCTION` | The product supports LIVE auctions only. |

`LIVE_AUCTION` is a response-only value and is not supported in the request bodies listed above.
#### What action is required
If your app uses these Product APIs, review the following updates.
Recommended actions:

* Update your request models to support the optional `selling_format` field and its two supported request values.
* Set `selling_format` to `FIXED_PRICE_AND_LIVE_AUCTION` when enabling both fixed-price sales and LIVE auctions, and ensure a starting bid price is provided as required.
* Update your Get Product and Search Products response models to handle all three response values, including `LIVE_AUCTION`.
* When reusing retrieved product data in creation, editing, or listing-check requests, ensure the response-only value `LIVE_AUCTION` is not passed as a request value.
* Test request handling for both supported request values and response handling for all three selling formats before deploying your integration changes.

## 2026-09-16 · Return and Refund · Cancel Order API: Two Buyer-Fault Cancellation Reasons Will Be Removed

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/i9eftnnv

#### Summary
Buyer-fault cancellation reasons have already been removed from the seller-initiated cancellation flow in Seller Center, for both standard orders and auction orders. API callers, however, can still attribute a cancellation to two buyer-fault reasons: **Buyer requested cancellation** and **Unable to deliver to buyer address**.
Starting **October 31, 2026**, these two reasons will also be removed from the API channel so that API behavior is aligned with Seller Center. From that date, requests using either value are rejected. This applies to **all markets**, for both **local-to-local and cross-border sellers**.
#### 1. Impact
| Item | Detail |
| --- | --- |
| **Impacted endpoint** | **Request (enum values removed)**: Cancel Order — `POST /return_refund/202602/cancellations`, field `cancel_reason` |
| **Impacted version(s)** | 202602. Seller cancellation reasons are validated at platform level and shared across Cancel Order versions, so apply the same change if your app still calls an earlier version |
| **Impacted market(s)** | US/UK/EU/JP |
| **Seller type(s) / App type(s)** | All seller types — both local-to-local and cross-border sellers. Applies to standard order and auction order cancellation flows |
| **Permission scope** | No new permission scope. Already-authorized apps do not need to be re-authorized |
| **Effective date** | **October 31, 2026** |
| **Compatibility** | **Breaking** — two existing enum values of `cancel_reason` are removed. Apps that hardcode either value will start receiving errors and will be unable to cancel those orders |

#### 2. What is changing
Seller-initiated cancellation reasons for paid orders (`ON_HOLD` / `AWAITING_SHIPMENT`):
| Before October 31, 2026 | After October 31, 2026 |
| --- | --- |
| ✅ Out of stock  `seller_cancel_reason_out_of_stock`  ✅ Pricing error  `seller_cancel_reason_wrong_price`  ❌ Unable to deliver to buyer address  `seller_cancel_paid_reason_address_not_deliver`  ❌ Buyer requested cancellation  `seller_cancel_paid_reason_buyer_requested_cancellation` | ✅ Out of stock  `seller_cancel_reason_out_of_stock`  ✅ Pricing error  `seller_cancel_reason_wrong_price`  ✅ **Seller unable to fulfill order**  `seller_cancel_reason_unable_to_fulfill_order`  The two buyer-fault reasons are no longer available. |
##### Field-level diff
| API | Location | Field | Change |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Cancel Order | Request body (root) | `cancel_reason` | **2 enum values removed**: `seller_cancel_paid_reason_address_not_deliver`, `seller_cancel_paid_reason_buyer_requested_cancellation`  **1 enum value available**: `seller_cancel_reason_unable_to_fulfill_order` |
##### Reason code reference
| Reason label | Reason code | From Oct 31, 2026 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Unable to deliver to buyer address | `seller_cancel_paid_reason_address_not_deliver` | **Removed** |
| Buyer requested cancellation | `seller_cancel_paid_reason_buyer_requested_cancellation` | **Removed** |
| Out of stock | `seller_cancel_reason_out_of_stock` | Available |
| Pricing error | `seller_cancel_reason_wrong_price` | Available |
| Seller unable to fulfill order | `seller_cancel_reason_unable_to_fulfill_order` | Available |

#### 3. Behavior after the effective date
If your app sends a removed reason code, the cancellation request fails. The documented error codes to handle are:
| Error code | Message |
| --- | --- |
| `25001020` | The provided return reason is currently offline. Please select a different return reason. |
| `25001015` | The provided return reason cannot be used by the seller. Please select a valid return reason and try again. |
| `25001014` | Unknown return reason. Please ensure you are passing a valid return reason. |

#### 4. Is this a breaking change?
**Yes.**

* **Request compatibility** — two accepted enum values of a required field (`cancel_reason`) are removed. Existing payloads using them stop working; the call fails rather than degrading silently.
* **Response compatibility** — no response field changes. The impact is on the request side and on any seller-facing UI that renders the removed reasons.
* **Authorization compatibility** — no new permission scope, no re-authorization needed.
* **Migration window** — both reasons remain accepted until **October 31, 2026**, so migration can be completed any time before that date.

#### 5. Developer action items
##### No action required

* Your app does not let sellers cancel orders.
* Your app never sends the two removed reasons. Verify by searching your codebase for `seller_cancel_paid_reason_address_not_deliver` and `seller_cancel_paid_reason_buyer_requested_cancellation`.

##### Action required before October 31, 2026
If your app sends either removed reason:

1. Remove both reasons from every seller-facing cancellation reason picker, mapping table and configuration.
2. Re-map affected cases to a valid reason: `seller_cancel_reason_out_of_stock` for stock issues, `seller_cancel_reason_wrong_price` for pricing errors, and `seller_cancel_reason_unable_to_fulfill_order` for cases previously attributed to the buyer.
3. Remove any default or fallback logic that automatically falls back to a removed reason.
4. Handle `25001020` / `25001015` / `25001014` as "reason no longer valid": prompt the seller to re-select a reason instead of retrying the same payload.

##### Recommended

* Stop hardcoding the reason list. Keep it configurable per market and drive it from the [Cancel reasons](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/cancel-reasons) documentation, since reason availability differs by market and order status.

#### References

* [Cancel Order](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/cancel-order-202602)
* [Cancel reasons](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/cancel-reasons)
* [Return, refund, and cancel API overview](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/return-refund-and-cancel-api-overview)

## 2026-09-10 · Other · Post Shoppable Photos and Post Shoppable Video APIs: Auction-Only Product Restriction Added

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/t9vttkxw

#### Summary
Post Shoppable Photos and Post Shoppable Video APIs now validate whether an attached product only supports auction sales. Auction-only products cannot be used in shoppable videos or carousels.
Both APIs return the new error code `170001063` when an auction-only product is added.
#### What is changing
The following validation and error code are added to both APIs.
| API | Validation |
| --- | --- |
| Post Shoppable Photos | Rejects auction-only products attached to shoppable carousels. |
| Post Shoppable Video | Rejects auction-only products attached to shoppable videos. |

| Error code | Error message |
| --- | --- |
| `170001063` | The product you added only supports auction sales and cannot be used in shoppable videos or Carousels |

#### What action is required
If your app uses Post Shoppable Photos or Post Shoppable Video, review the following updates.
Recommended actions:

* Update your error handling to recognize `170001063` and explain the product restriction to users.
* Prompt users to replace the auction-only product with another eligible product before retrying.
* Test the error handling for auction-only products before deploying your integration changes.

## 2026-09-10 · Products · Get Opportunity Detail and List Opportunity APIs: New Fields Added

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/57me406k

#### Summary
Get Opportunity Detail and List Opportunity APIs add new response fields to provide opportunity tag codes and reference product information. Get Opportunity Detail also adds opportunity tags, while List Opportunity adds information about rewards sellers can receive for listing products as required.
List Opportunity also supports filtering opportunities by tag codes through a new optional request field, `tag_codes`.
#### What is changing
**Get Opportunity Detail — 4 new response fields**
| Field | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `tags` | String | Tags associated with the opportunity. |
| `tag_codes` | List<String> | Tag codes mapped one-to-one, in order, to `tags`. Supported values for LATAM include `CORE_PRODUCT` and `TOP_PRODUCT`. |
| `external_product_id` | String | Product ID on the reference source. **-Only for LATAM** |
| `reference_source` | String | Reference source code. Returned only when `external_product_id` is present. **-Only for LATAM** |

**List Opportunity — 1 new request field**
| Field | Type | Required | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `tag_codes` | List<String> | No | Filters opportunities by tag codes. Example: `["CORE_PRODUCT", "TOP_PRODUCT"]`. |

**List Opportunity — 4 new response fields under** **`data.opportunities[]`**
| Field | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `tag_codes` | List<String> | Tag codes mapped one-to-one, in order, to `tags`. Supported values for LATAM include `CORE_PRODUCT` and `TOP_PRODUCT`. |
| `reward_info` | String | Rewards sellers can receive for listing products as required. |
| `external_product_id` | String | Product ID on the reference source. **-Only for LATAM** |
| `reference_source` | String | Reference source code. Returned only when `external_product_id` is present. **-Only for LATAM** |
#### What action is required
If your app uses Get Opportunity Detail or List Opportunity, review the following updates.
Recommended actions:

* Update your response models to support the new fields. Note that `tags` is a string, while `tag_codes` is an array of strings.
* Include the optional `tag_codes` request field in List Opportunity when filtering opportunities by tag.
* Use `reward_info` to display available listing rewards, and `external_product_id` together with `reference_source` to identify reference products.
* Handle responses where `reference_source` is absent.
* Test the updated request and response handling before deploying your integration changes.

## 2026-09-10 · Fulfillment · For UK and EU Markets: Shipped by Platform - Edit Parcel Dimensions and Weight for Shipping Label Creation

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/tovst8vl

### What is changing?
This update expands the existing **Create Packages** endpoint to **UK and EU** sellers and ISVs. Previously, this endpoint was used by US sellers and ISVs; With this change, UK/EU integrations can use it for shipping label creation and package submission flows.
For the updated fulfillment flow, refer to the [Fulfillment API Overview](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/fulfillment-api-overview), specifically the **Purchase shipping** diagram.
The change responds to shipping issues in Shipped by Platform (4PL orders), where auction-driven package consolidation and inaccurate product sizes can create shipping fee deviations. Sellers will be able to modify package size and weight during the shipping process, while Box Management supports commonly used box types and default box selection. The expected outcome is to reduce shipping fee deviations, merchant disputes, and carrier chargebacks.
#### Affected API endpoints
| API | Change |
| --- | --- |
| [Create Packages](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/create-packages-202512)  /fulfillment/202512/packages | Existing endpoint is now supported for **UK/EU** shipping flows. Request supports passing **dimension** and **weight** per order/line item; the platform uses the latest values for routing and shipping label purchase. |
#### Request parameters (Create Packages)
The following request fields should be used when passing seller-edited package dimensions and weight:
| Field | Type | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| data.orders[].line_items[].dimension.length | string | Package length. **Length/width/height must be passed together.** |
| data.orders[].line_items[].dimension.width | string | Package width. **Length/width/height must be passed together.** |
| data.orders[].line_items[].dimension.height | string | Package height. **Length/width/height must be passed together.** |
| data.orders[].line_items[].dimension.unit | string | Allowed: CM, INCH. **EU: use CM.** |
| data.orders[].line_items[].weight.value | number | Numerical value of package weight. |
| data.orders[].line_items[].weight.unit | string | Allowed: GRAM, POUND. **EU: use GRAM (g).** |
### Which markets are affected?
This change applies to **EU and UK** fulfillment/shipping flows.
### Who is affected?
ISVs whose integrations cover any of the following in EU/UK:

* **Shipping label download / package creation** via Open API
* **Order fulfillment operations** that need to reflect the seller’s latest packaging info

### **What action is required?**

1. **Update your Shipped by Platform (4PL) fulfillment flow** to support the Create Packages endpoint for UK/EU shipping label creation and package submission. **++This is not a required flow change — ISVs should implement this as a configurable option++**, choosing to either pass product-level dimensions and weight directly without seller confirmation, or present sellers with a review and edit step for length, width, height, and weight before creating the shipping label. Sellers with consistently accurate product dimensions may not need the additional confirmation step.
2. **Pass dimensions and weight** in the Create Packages request, using CM for dimensions and GRAM for weight in EU/UK flows.
3. **Validate complete package data:** when sending dimensions, always provide length + width + height together, and keep the values available for fulfillment, re-ship, dispute, and carrier chargeback workflows.

## 2026-09-10 · Other · Webhook Egress IP Update

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/iuhsev4x

**Tag**: `Action required` · 

* **Change type**: no change to the webhook contract 
* **Effective date**: **2026.8.30**
* **What changed**: new egress (source) IP ranges are being added to the infrastructure that delivers TikTok Shop webhooks.
* **Who must act**: apps whose webhook endpoint only accepts inbound traffic from an allowlist of TikTok Shop source IPs.
* **If you do nothing**: webhooks sent from the new IPs are dropped by your own firewall, so you **silently miss events** — no error is returned to you.
* **Not affected**: payloads, event types, headers, signature verification, retry behavior, and all webhook subscription APIs are unchanged.

#### Summary
TikTok Shop is adding new egress IP ranges to the infrastructure that sends webhook notifications to your endpoint. All previously published egress IPs remain valid — the entries below are additions, not replacements.
If your webhook endpoint sits behind a firewall, WAF, security group, load balancer, API gateway, or reverse proxy that filters inbound traffic by source IP, add the new ranges before the effective date. This is a network configuration change; no application code change is required.

#### Impact
| Item | Detail |
| --- | --- |
| Impacted surface | Inbound webhook delivery to your registered webhook URL, for all event types. No REST API request or response is affected. |
| Impacted endpoints | None. |
| Impacted region(s) | Webhook traffic sent from the **SG** and **EU** egress regions.   US egress IPs are unchanged. |
| Impacted developer(s)   | Any app — public or custom, local-to-local or cross-border — whose webhook endpoint enforces an inbound source-IP allowlist. Apps without IP filtering are unaffected. |
| Permission scope | No new permission scope. No re-authorization required. |
| Effective date | **To be confirmed** — the date from which webhook traffic may originate from the new ranges. |
| Compatibility | Webhook payloads, headers, signature verification and retry behavior are unchanged. The only required action is a network allowlist update. |

#### Newly added egress IP ranges
| Region | Newly added egress IPs |
| --- | --- |
| Southeast Asia (SG) | `18.99.47.0/24` (`18.99.47.0` – `18.99.47.255`)  `18.97.10.0/24` (`18.97.10.0` – `18.97.10.255`) |
| Europe | `18.99.98.0/24` (`18.99.98.0` – `18.99.98.255`)  `101.45.244.151`  `101.45.244.164`  `101.45.244.138`  `101.45.244.25` |
#### Full webhook egress IP allowlist after this update
Use this as the complete list when configuring your allowlist. Entries marked **New** are the additions introduced by this update.
| Region | Webhook egress IPs |
| --- | --- |
| United States | `130.44.215.160`  `130.44.215.169`  `139.177.251.0/24`  `139.177.252.0/24` |
| Southeast Asia (SG) | `103.136.220.238`  `103.136.220.239`  `103.136.221.238`  `103.136.221.239`  `18.99.47.0/24` — **New**  `18.97.10.0/24` — **New** |
| Europe | `34.85.199.116`  `35.245.18.163`  `34.86.242.105`  `34.145.216.219`  `3.254.9.0/24`  `18.99.98.0/24` — **New**  `101.45.244.151` — **New**  `101.45.244.164` — **New**  `101.45.244.138` — **New**  `101.45.244.25` — **New** |

Allowlist by CIDR wherever a CIDR block is published. Do not allowlist only the individual addresses you have observed in your traffic logs — any address inside a published range may be used. Webhook traffic for a shop is sent from the region serving that shop's market, so if your app serves shops in several markets, allowlist every region you operate in. When in doubt, allowlist the full list above.

#### Is this a breaking change?
Not a breaking change to the webhook contract, but **action is required** for apps that filter by source IP.

* **Payload and contract**: unchanged. No webhook field, event type, header or signature scheme is added, removed or modified.
* **Authorization**: unchanged. No new permission scope, no re-authorization.
* **Delivery**: impacted for IP-allowlisting apps. Requests from the new ranges are blocked by your own infrastructure rather than rejected by TikTok Shop, so there is no error signal on either side — only missing events.

#### Developer action items

* **If your endpoint accepts webhook traffic from any IP** (no firewall, WAF or gateway allowlist): no action is required.
* **If you enforce an inbound source-IP allowlist**: add every range in the full allowlist table to your firewall, WAF, security group, load balancer, API gateway and reverse proxy rules before the effective date. Add CIDR blocks as CIDR, not as individual addresses.
* **If your network rules are managed by a cloud provider or hosting vendor**: forward this notice to whoever owns those rules — the change sits at the network layer, not in your application code.
* **After updating**: verify delivery instead of assuming success. Confirm that you still receive events for every market you serve, and check your firewall drop logs for denied requests from the new ranges.
* **Recommended hardening**: treat webhook signature verification as your primary authenticity check and IP allowlisting as defense in depth. Signature verification is unaffected by infrastructure IP changes.

Questions or delivery issues after updating your allowlist: contact TikTok Shop developer support at support-us@tiktok.com.


---

## 2026-09-09 · Products · Combined Listing: Merge into Variants and API Integration Updates

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/k0ubfjdt

Combined Listing now supports **Merge into Variants**, allowing sellers to consolidate same-style products listed under separate links into one main product detail page. New sales and reviews generated through the main listing are aggregated under the main product. Historical sales and reviews are not fully transferred, and orders placed through the original child listings continue to contribute to those child products. [Feature guide](https://seller-us.tiktok.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=1016792318773006)
**This feature uses the existing virtual bundle response structure. ISVs should review how they map main listing SKUs to original child SKUs.** Merged child products receive newly generated SKUs under the main product, while their original SKU IDs are returned in the component SKU fields:
| API | Main listing SKU ID | Original child SKU ID |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Get Product | `data.skus[].id` | `data.skus[].combined_skus[].sku_id` |
| Get Order Detail | `data.orders[].line_items[].sku_id` | `data.orders[].line_items[].combined_listing_skus[].sku_id` |

ISVs should use [Get Product](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-product-202309) to synchronize SKU mappings and read `combined_listing_skus` in [Get Order Detail](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-order-detail-202309) to identify the original `product_id`, `sku_id`, `seller_sku`, and component quantity (`sku_count`) for fulfillment. **Integrations that rely solely on the order line’s** **`sku_id` to identify the original child SKU require adaptation.** Validate SKU mapping, order synchronization, and fulfillment before supporting merged listings.
The following seller and product restrictions apply:

* **Seller availability:** This announcement applies to US sellers with the feature enabled. Availability is subject to the feature being accessible in Seller Center.
* **Brand and category:** Select at least two same-style products from the **same brand and the same leaf category**.
* **Product types:** Only regular products are supported. Flash sale products, auction products, and certain special distribution, GPR, or POP scenario products are excluded. Eligibility is determined by the validation shown in Seller Center.
* **Existing relationships and SKU limits:** Products must not already belong to another active product combination or combined listing relationship. Remove any existing relationship first. The total SKU count across selected products must remain within the platform limit. [Full eligibility rules](https://seller-us.tiktok.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=1016792318773006)

Sellers can configure the feature in **Seller Center → Manage Products → Combined Listing → Merge into Variants**. Prices and inventory for SKUs linked to child products are synchronized from the original child SKUs; updates should be made to those original child products. [Setup guide](https://seller-us.tiktok.com/university/essay?knowledge_id=1016792318773006)

## 2026-09-05 · Promotion · For All markets：New auction_support_type Field to Control Auction Product Support

Doc: https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/xiqhn3o4

### **Summary**
The Promotion Activity APIs add one optional field, `auction_support_type`, which declares whether a promotion activity applies to auction products in addition to normal products. It is accepted as an optional request field on Create / Update Promotion Activity and returned on Get Activity / Search Promotion Activities.
#### Impact
| Item | Detail |
| --- | --- |
| Impacted endpoints | **Request (new optional field)**:   Create Promotion Activity (`POST /promotion/202309/activities`),   Update Promotion Activity (`PUT /promotion/202309/activities/{activity_id}`)  **Response (new field)**:   Get Activity (`GET /promotion/202309/activities/{activity_id}`),   Search Promotion Activities (`POST /promotion/202309/activities/search`) |
| Impacted version(s) | 202309 |
| Impacted market(s) | All markets |
| Seller type(s) / App type(s) | All seller types — both local-to-local and cross-border sellers |
| Permission scope | No new permission scope. Existing Promotion scopes are reused, and already-authorized apps do not need to be re-authorized |
| Effective date | **Already live** — the field is available now, no migration window |
| Compatibility | **Non-breaking.** Only one optional request field and one response field are added; no field, enum value or behavior is removed or changed |

#### Field-level diff
| API | Field | Old | New |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Create Promotion Activity | `auction_support_type` | Not available | `string`, **optional** (`Require = N`), default `ALL`, enum `ALL` / `EXCLUDE_AUCTION` |
| Update Promotion Activity | `auction_support_type` | Not available | `string`, **optional** (`Require = N`), default `ALL`, enum `ALL` / `EXCLUDE_AUCTION` |
| Get Activity | `auction_support_type` | Not returned | `string`, returned with the activity detail, value `ALL` / `EXCLUDE_AUCTION` |
| Search Promotion Activities | `auction_support_type` | Not returned | `string`, returned per activity in the list, value `ALL` / `EXCLUDE_AUCTION` |
##### Field definition
`auction_support_type` (`string`, optional, default `ALL`) determines whether the activity supports auction products.

* `ALL`: the activity supports both normal products and auction products.
* `EXCLUDE_AUCTION`: the activity does not support auction products.

##### Samples
```JSON
{
  "title": "DiscountEvent0829",
  "activity_type": "FIXED_PRICE",
  "product_level": "PRODUCT",
  "auction_support_type": "ALL"
}
```

```JSON
{
  "code": 0,
  "data": {
    "activity_id": "7136104329798256386",
    "activity_type": "FIXED_PRICE",
    "auction_support_type": "ALL"
  },
  "message": "Success"
}
```

#### Is this a breaking change?
**No** — this is a **non-breaking, additive change**.

* The new request field is optional. Existing request payloads remain valid, and omitting it keeps the current behavior (`ALL`).
* The new response field is additive. No existing field is removed, renamed or re-typed.
* No new permission scope is introduced, so no re-authorization is required.

#### Developer action items

* **If you do not manage auction products**: no action is required. Existing calls keep working with the default `ALL`.
* **If you need to exclude auction products from an activity**: pass `auction_support_type = EXCLUDE_AUCTION` on Create Promotion Activity, or update an existing activity through Update Promotion Activity.
* **If your app has a strict response schema**: add `auction_support_type` to your activity models for Get Activity and Search Promotion Activities, treat the field as optional, and tolerate unknown future enum values instead of failing the parse.
* Surface the value in any UI that displays activity eligibility, so sellers can tell whether an activity covers auction products.

#### References

* [Create Promotion Activity](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/create-promotion-activity-202309)
* [Update Promotion Activity](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/update-promotion-activity-202309)
* [Get Activity](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/get-activity-202309)
* [Search Promotion Activities](https://partner.tiktokshop.com/docv2/page/search-promotion-activities-202309)
